================================================================================
PART 1: WHAT WORKS & PASSED VERIFICATION IN PHASE 2
================================================================================

1. Bit-Identical Fixed-Order Legacy Parity (Commit bacb2ff)
   - The 7 extracted processors (LegacyHpfProcessor, LegacyNoiseProcessor,
     LegacyGateProcessor, LegacyToneProcessor, LegacyDeEsserProcessor,
     LegacyCompressorProcessor, LegacyLimiterProcessor) and the percentage-to-
     fraction scaling fix in commit bacb2ff (amt01(), warmth01(), presence01(),
     drive01() dividing raw 0..100 values by 100f) reproduce the pre-Stage-2
     DspChain float output bit-for-bit when executed in the default canonical
     order.
   - Partial 16-frame tail blocks (e.g., 4801 frames) and mid-stream live
     parameter updates preserve biquad z1/z2 and envelope state identically to
     the pre-Stage-2 engine.

2. Stage 1 Seam Crossfades, Pre-Roll Warm-Up & Save Guard
   - EditEngine.applyDspToRange() correctly warms up the DSP chain on 240 frames
     (5 ms @ 48 kHz) of audio preceding the selection start and applies 240-frame
     raised-cosine head and tail crossfades using global frame offsets across
     chunk boundaries.
   - ConversionJobs.dispatchSave() and EditEngine.isFxBakeRequired() properly
     prevent adopting an unbaked incremental part when an active FX chain has
     not yet been committed to raw.wav.

3. Standalone FxRackChain & FxParameter Serialization
   - FxRackChain.toJson() / fromJson() and AudioProcessor.saveState() /
     loadState() preserve processor order, enabled state, wet/dry mix, and
     parameter raw values, while safely skipping unknown processor IDs for
     forward compatibility.


================================================================================
PART 2: EXHAUSTIVE EXPLANATION OF ALL 10 BUGS FOUND (ZERO-LENIENCY AUDIT)
================================================================================

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
BUG-01 [CRITICAL]: UI Slot Reordering ("Move Up" / "Move Down") Has Zero Effect
                   on Audio Processing Order
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Files & Lines:
  * StudioFragment.java : lines 3006-3021
  * DspChain.java       : lines 554-587
  * FxRackChain.java    : lines 79-89, 219-280
- Plan Requirement Violated:
  * Section 1 Bug #4 of takeone_pro_audio_effects_suite_plan.txt states:
    "Reordering slots in dspConfig.slots has zero effect on audio processing
    because DspChain.processInPlace() executes a fixed 7-stage hardcoded
    sequence."
  * Section 3 Stage 2 requires wiring "Move Up / Move Down" in StudioFragment's
    FX rack to actually reorder the execution sequence in FxRackChain.
- Root Cause:
  * In StudioFragment.rebuildFxRackList() (lines 3006-3021), tapping "Move Up"
    or "Move Down" mutates dspConfig.slots and calls onSlotMutated.run(), which
    invokes livePreviewDsp.updateConfig(dspConfig).
  * However, DspChain.updateConfig(cfg) only calls rack.applyLegacyConfig(cfg)
    and applySlotExtras(cfg). Neither method reorders rack.processors!
  * FxRackChain.fromLegacyConfig() adds the 7 processors in the fixed order
    [hpf, noise, gate, tone, deess, comp, limiter], and FxRackChain.moveProcessor()
    (line 79) is never called anywhere in production code (only in a standalone
    unit test).
- Audio/User Impact:
  * Moving Compressor before Warmth/Presence or moving Low-Cut HPF after
    Compressor in the UI visually reorders the cards and persists the new card
    order in dsp_slots_json, but both live preview and offline "Apply to Take"
    still run the fixed [hpf -> noise -> gate -> tone -> deess -> comp -> limiter]
    order.
- Fix Applied:
  * Added FxRackChain.processorIdForSlotType(EffectType) and
    FxRackChain.syncOrderFromSlots(DspChain.Config), called inside
    FxRackChain.applyLegacyConfig(cfg).
  * It maps each slot in cfg.slots to its processor ID, preserves existing
    AudioProcessor instances (and their live filter/envelope state) by calling
    moveProcessor(curIdx, targetIdx), and keeps any unreferenced stages (such
    as the automatic safety limiter when no explicit limiter slot was moved
    earlier) in canonical order at the tail of the chain.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
BUG-02 [CRITICAL]: Brickwall Limiter Renders a Duplicate "Drive" Knob That
                   Overwrites and Breaks the Primary "Drive" Control
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Files & Lines:
  * StudioFragment.java         : lines 2804-2806, 3072-3126
  * LegacyLimiterProcessor.java : lines 14-15
  * DspChain.java               : lines 567-588
- Root Cause:
  * LegacyLimiterProcessor declares its primary parameter with ID "drive":
      addParameter(FxParameter.intParam("drive", "Drive", "%", 0, 100, 1, 50));
  * In StudioFragment.java (lines 2804-2806), isPrimaryFxParam(String paramId)
    only checks:
      return "amt".equals(paramId) || "cutoff".equals(paramId);
    It forgot "drive"!
  * As a result, when a user adds or expands Brickwall Ceiling Limiter in the
    FX rack, rebuildFxRackList() renders:
      1) The primary TeleValue ("Drive", bound to slot.value), AND
      2) A second duplicate TeleValue ("Drive", bound to
         slot.extraParams.put("drive", val))!
  * Even worse: in DspChain.updateConfig(cfg), applySlotExtras(cfg) runs AFTER
    rack.applyLegacyConfig(cfg) and copies slot.extraParams into the processor's
    FxParameters. Once the duplicate "Drive" knob is touched, slot.extraParams
    contains "drive", which overwrites the value set by slot.value on every
    subsequent updateConfig() call, rendering the primary Drive control broken!
- Fix Applied:
  * Updated StudioFragment.isPrimaryFxParam(String) to include
    "drive".equals(paramId).
  * Hardened DspChain.applySlotExtras(Config) to skip primary legacy parameter
    IDs ("amt", "cutoff", "drive", "warmth", "presence") when applying
    slot.extraParams so stale extraParams can never clobber slot.value.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
BUG-03 [HIGH]: Brickwall Limiter Wet/Dry Mix Defeats the Automatic Safety
               Ceiling & Allows Hard Digital Clipping
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Files & Lines:
  * FxRackChain.java            : lines 147-171, 270-278
  * DspChain.java               : lines 577-588
  * LegacyLimiterProcessor.java : lines 49-92
- Root Cause:
  * LegacyLimiterProcessor serves two roles: (1) an explicit +10.5 dB loudness
    drive effect when EffectType.BRICKWALL_LIMITER is enabled, and (2) the
    automatic brickwall safety ceiling (-0.2 dBFS soft-clip above 0.82f)
    whenever Compressor, Warmth, or Vocal Presence is active.
  * Two defects interact here:
    1) In DspChain.applySlotExtras(cfg), p.setWetDryMix(s.wetDry) is pushed to
       LegacyLimiterProcessor even when the BRICKWALL_LIMITER slot is unchecked
       (!s.enabled). If a user lowers the limiter's Wet-Dry Mix and then
       unchecks the limiter slot, the safety limiter's mix remains < 1.0f!
    2) Even when the BRICKWALL_LIMITER slot is enabled, if the user lowers its
       Wet-Dry Mix (e.g., to 0% or 30% to reduce the +10.5 dB drive),
       FxRackChain.processInPlace() either skips the limiter completely (when
       mix == 0f) or blends un-limited dry samples from upstream gain stages
       (Warmth up to +9 dB, Presence up to +10 dB, Compressor makeup gain),
       allowing samples to exceed 1.0f and hard-clip!
- Fix Applied:
  * In DspChain.applySlotExtras(cfg), only apply s.wetDry to BRICKWALL_LIMITER
    when s.enabled is true (unchecked limiter slot leaves safety mix at 1.0f).
  * Added LegacyLimiterProcessor.isSafetyOn() and
    LegacyLimiterProcessor.enforceSafetyCeiling(buf, frames, channels).
  * In FxRackChain.processInPlace(), whenever LegacyLimiterProcessor has
    isSafetyOn() == true and mix < 1.0f, enforceSafetyCeiling() clamps the
    post-blend buffer to the unity-gain -0.2 dBFS ceiling so lowering the
    limiter's mix blends its loudness drive without ever defeating peak safety.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
BUG-04 [HIGH]: Deleting an Effect Slot Leaves Stale Wet/Dry Mix & Extra
               Parameters on the Processor and Misses dspBakedToRaw = false
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Files & Lines:
  * DspChain.java       : lines 567-588
  * StudioFragment.java : lines 2946-2955
- Root Cause:
  * In DspChain.applySlotExtras(cfg), the loop only iterates over slots
    currently present in cfg.slots. If a user sets a slot's Wet-Dry Mix to 20%
    (or adjusts a secondary parameter) and then deletes that slot from the
    rack, cfg.slots no longer contains that slot, so applySlotExtras(cfg)
    never resets the backing processor's wetDryMix back to 1.0f or its
    secondary FxParameters back to defaults!
  * Furthermore, in StudioFragment.rebuildFxRackList() (lines 2946-2955),
    delBtn.setOnClickListener removes the slot and updates livePreviewDsp, but
    forgets to set dspBakedToRaw = false; (unlike onSlotMutated at line 2917).
- Fix Applied:
  * Added DspChain.resetProcessorSlotExtras() and called it at the start of
    DspChain.updateConfig(cfg) so all processors reset wetDryMix = 1.0f and
    secondary parameters to defaults before applying the current cfg.slots.
  * Added dspBakedToRaw = false; in delBtn.setOnClickListener in
    StudioFragment.java.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
BUG-05 [HIGH]: Config.syncSlotsFromLegacyFields() Wipes Out Per-Slot wetDry,
               extraParams, and Custom Slot Ordering
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Files & Lines:
  * DspChain.java : lines 325-352, 354-414
- Root Cause:
  * Config.syncSlotsFromLegacyFields() unconditionally calls slots.clear() and
    constructs brand-new EffectSlot instances with default wetDry = 1.0f, empty
    extraParams, expanded = false, and a hardcoded insertion order (which also
    placed DE_ESSER and COMPRESSOR before WARMTH_BODY, VOCAL_PRESENCE, and
    ROOM_GATE, contrary to the canonical signal chain order).
  * Calling onManualAdjustment() or applyMode() therefore destroys all per-slot
    wet/dry mixes, secondary parameter values, expanded drawer states, and
    user-customized slot ordering.
- Fix Applied:
  * Updated Config.syncSlotsFromLegacyFields() to snapshot existing EffectSlot
    instances by EffectType and their current relative order before rebuilding
    slots, reusing existing EffectSlot instances (preserving wetDry,
    extraParams, expanded, and user ordering) and appending any newly enabled
    stages in canonical DSP stage order (LOW_CUT_HPF, NOISE_SUPPRESSION,
    ROOM_GATE, WARMTH_BODY, VOCAL_PRESENCE, DE_ESSER, COMPRESSOR,
    BRICKWALL_LIMITER).

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
BUG-06 [MEDIUM-HIGH]: FX Inspector Ignores ENUM & ACTION Parameters, Uses Wrong
                      Default for BOOLEAN, and Fails to Wire formatDisplay()
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Files & Lines:
  * StudioFragment.java : lines 3072-3127
- Root Cause:
  1) BOOLEAN Default Bug (lines 3078-3080):
       final boolean on = slot.extraParams.containsKey(param.id)
               && slot.extraParams.get(param.id) != 0;
     When a BOOLEAN parameter has defaultRaw == 1 (true) and the user has not
     yet tapped the chip (so slot.extraParams does not contain param.id), 'on'
     evaluates to false ("Off") in the UI even though the processor's parameter
     is actually true ("On")!
  2) ENUM & ACTION Parameters Skipped (lines 3091-3094):
     Any parameter whose type is Type.ENUM or Type.ACTION hits 'continue;' and
     is completely invisible in the FX inspector.
  3) FxParameter.formatDisplay() Not Wired to TeleValue (lines 3095-3102):
     Secondary INT and FLOAT TeleValue controls set pc.setValueScale(...) and
     pass param.unit, but never call pc.setValueFormatter(...). Consequently,
     Scale.DECIBEL signed formatting ("%+.1f dB") and custom
     FxParameter.ValueFormatter callbacks are bypassed by TeleValue.
- Fix Applied:
  * In StudioFragment.rebuildFxRackList():
    - BOOLEAN chips now read slot.extraParams.getOrDefault(param.id,
      param.defaultRaw) != 0.
    - ENUM parameters render an interactive chip ("Name: Label") that cycles
      through param.enumLabels on tap and persists the index in
      slot.extraParams.
    - ACTION parameters render an interactive action chip that increments an
      action trigger counter in slot.extraParams and triggers onSlotMutated.
    - INT and FLOAT TeleValue controls wire pc.setValueFormatter(...) to
      param.formatDisplay().

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
BUG-07 [MEDIUM]: FxParameter.clampRaw() Truncates Instead of Rounding to Nearest
                 Step and Can Make maxRaw Unreachable
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Files & Lines:
  * FxParameter.java : lines 116-123
- Root Cause:
  * clampRaw(int raw) computes:
      if (stepRaw > 1) {
          v = minRaw + ((v - minRaw) / stepRaw) * stepRaw;
      }
  * Because integer division truncates toward zero:
    - Calling setFloat() or setCurrentRaw(48) on a parameter with min=0,
      max=100, step=5 truncates 48 down to 45 instead of rounding to the
      nearest step (50).
    - If (maxRaw - minRaw) is not an exact multiple of stepRaw, clamping
      raw = maxRaw truncates down to maxRaw - ((maxRaw - minRaw) % stepRaw),
      making maxRaw completely unreachable.
- Fix Applied:
  * Updated FxParameter.clampRaw(int raw) to preserve maxRaw when v == maxRaw
    and to round to the nearest step via
    Math.round((v - minRaw) / (float) stepRaw) * stepRaw.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
BUG-08 [MEDIUM]: TeleValue Negative Value Bugs in formatNumberPart() and
                 steppedValues()
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Files & Lines:
  * TeleValue.java : lines 114-136, 365-398
- Root Cause:
  1) In formatNumberPart(int v) (line 122):
       if (v >= 10 * valueScale)
     checks signed v instead of Math.abs((long) v). For negative scaled values
     such as -12.5 dB (v = -1250, valueScale = 100), v >= 1000 is false, so
     negative values >= 10 in magnitude fall through to the 2-decimal branch
     asymmetrically.
  2) In steppedValues() (line 384):
       int first = Math.min(max, ((min + step - 1) / step) * step);
     In Java, integer division rounds toward zero (not floor). When min < 0
     (e.g., min = -2400, step = 600, or min = -10, step = 5), (min + step - 1)
     is -1801 (or -6), and (-1801 / 600) * 600 evaluates to -1800 (or -5),
     skipping min (-2400 or -10) when valueScale == 1!
- Fix Applied:
  * Used Math.abs((long) v) >= 10L * valueScale in formatNumberPart(int v).
  * Computed non-negative remainder rem = ((min % step) + step) % step in
    steppedValues() so negative min values align accurately and min is never
    omitted from the NumberPicker wheel.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
BUG-09 [MEDIUM]: Missing volatile Visibility on AudioProcessor.enabled,
                 AudioProcessor.wetDryMix, and FxParameter.currentRaw
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Files & Lines:
  * AudioProcessor.java : lines 22-23
  * FxParameter.java    : lines 43-44
- Root Cause:
  * During live audio preview, RawFloatPlayer runs FxRackChain.processInPlace()
    on the background audio thread ("takeone-raw-preview") while the UI thread
    mutates AudioProcessor.enabled, AudioProcessor.wetDryMix, and
    FxParameter.currentRaw via TeleValue scrubs and chip toggles.
  * While FxRackChain.processors is a CopyOnWriteArrayList, the fields inside
    AudioProcessor (enabled, wetDryMix) and FxParameter (currentRaw, formatter)
    lacked the volatile modifier, allowing the ARM/ART JIT to hoist or cache
    stale values on the audio thread.
- Fix Applied:
  * Marked enabled and wetDryMix as volatile in AudioProcessor.java, and marked
    currentRaw and formatter as volatile in FxParameter.java.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
BUG-10 [LOW]: Unclamped Channel Count in Legacy*Processor.processInPlace() and
              Ignored sampleRate in prepare()
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Files & Lines:
  * LegacyHpfProcessor.java        : lines 25-38, 60-63, 90-94
  * LegacyDeEsserProcessor.java    : lines 25-35, 58-60, 88-92
  * LegacyNoiseProcessor.java      : lines 85-89
  * LegacyGateProcessor.java       : lines 66-70
  * LegacyToneProcessor.java       : lines 77-81
  * LegacyCompressorProcessor.java : lines 66-70
  * LegacyLimiterProcessor.java    : lines 64-68
- Root Cause:
  * All 7 Legacy*Processor classes allocate fixed 2-channel state arrays
    (new float[2], new float[DECIM * 2]), but processInPlace(buf, frames,
    channels) used 'channels' directly without clamping to [1, 2] or guarding
    against buf == null. Calling a processor directly with channels > 2 would
    throw ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException.
  * Additionally, FxRackChain.setSampleRate(int sr) calls
    p.prepare(sampleRate, channels) on every processor, but
    LegacyHpfProcessor and LegacyDeEsserProcessor ignored the sampleRate
    argument and hardcoded DspChain.SAMPLE_RATE (48000) in their biquad
    coefficient formulas.
- Fix Applied:
  * Clamped final int ch = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, channels)) and added a
    buf == null guard in all 7 Legacy*Processor.processInPlace() methods.
  * Updated LegacyHpfProcessor and LegacyDeEsserProcessor to store
    currentSampleRate (defaulting to DspChain.SAMPLE_RATE = 48000 for 100%
    bit-identical default behavior) and recompute biquad coefficients using
    the sampleRate passed to prepare(int sampleRate, int channels).

+    /** Maps a {@link DspChain.EffectType} to its rack processor ID. */
+    static String processorIdForSlotType(DspChain.EffectType type) {

+    /**
+     * Synchronizes the rack's processor execution order with the user's
+     * {@code cfg.slots} order while preserving existing {@link AudioProcessor}
+     * instances and their internal filter/envelope state. Unreferenced
+     * processors keep their canonical relative order (with the safety limiter
+     * anchored at the end unless explicitly moved earlier in {@code cfg.slots}).
+     */
+    private void syncOrderFromSlots(DspChain.Config cfg) {
+        if (cfg == null || cfg.slots == null || cfg.slots.isEmpty()) return;
+        Set<String> desiredIds = new LinkedHashSet<>();
+        for (DspChain.EffectSlot s : cfg.slots) {
+            if (s == null || s.type == null) continue;
+            String pid = processorIdForSlotType(s.type);
+            if (pid != null && indexOf(pid) >= 0) {
+                desiredIds.add(pid);
+            }
+        }
+        for (String canonId : CANONICAL_LEGACY_ORDER) {
+            if (indexOf(canonId) >= 0) {
+                desiredIds.add(canonId);
+            }
+        }
+        for (AudioProcessor p : processors) {
+            desiredIds.add(p.getId());
+        }
+        int targetIdx = 0;
+        for (String pid : desiredIds) {
+            int curIdx = indexOf(pid);
+            if (curIdx >= 0 && curIdx != targetIdx) {
+                moveProcessor(curIdx, targetIdx);
+            }
+            if (curIdx >= 0) {
+                targetIdx++;
+            }
+        }
+    }

     /** Raw drive is a percent; gain math needs a 0..1 fraction. */
     private float drive01() {

+    /**
+     * Applies only the unity-gain -0.2 dBFS brickwall safety ceiling without
+     * additional drive, used when the limiter's wet/dry mix is below 1.0 while
+     * upstream gain stages require peak protection.
+     */
+    void enforceSafetyCeiling(float[] buf, int frames, int channels) {
+        if (buf == null || frames <= 0) return;
+        final int ch = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, channels));
+        int n = Math.min(buf.length, frames * ch);
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+            float y = buf[i];
+            float ay = Math.abs(y);
+            if (ay > 0.82f) {
+                float d = ay - 0.82f;
+                float lim = 0.82f + 0.165f * (d / (0.165f + d));
+                buf[i] = y >= 0f ? lim : -lim;
+            }
+        }
+    }

+                < chainA.getRack().indexOf("comp"));
+
+        // Swap slot order in-place (Compressor BEFORE Warmth) and call updateConfig
+        DspChain.Config cfgB = new DspChain.Config();
+        cfgB.applyPreset(DspChain.Preset.CLEAR_ALL);
+        cfgB.addOrFocusSlot(DspChain.EffectType.COMPRESSOR);
+        cfgB.addOrFocusSlot(DspChain.EffectType.WARMTH_BODY);
+        for (DspChain.EffectSlot s : cfgB.slots) {
+            if (s.type == DspChain.EffectType.WARMTH_BODY) s.value = 85;
+            if (s.type == DspChain.EffectType.COMPRESSOR) s.value = 85;
+        }
+        DspChain chainB = new DspChain(1, cfgB);
+        assertTrue(chainB.getRack().indexOf("comp")
+                < chainB.getRack().indexOf("tone"));
+
+        float[] sigA = tone(1024, 1, 0.6f, 0.05f);
+        float[] sigB = sigA.clone();
+        chainA.processInPlace(sigA, 1024);
+        chainB.processInPlace(sigB, 1024);
+
+        boolean diff = false;
+        for (int i = 0; i < sigA.length; i++) {
+            if (Math.abs(sigA[i] - sigB[i]) > 1e-4f) {
+                diff = true;
+                break;
+            }
+        }
+        assertTrue("Reordering compressor before tone had no effect on audio", diff);
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    public void primarySlotParamCannotBeOverwrittenByStaleExtraParams() {
+        // BUG-02 regression: LegacyLimiterProcessor's primary "drive" param
+        // must be driven by slot.value and never clobbered by slot.extraParams.
+        DspChain.Config cfg = new DspChain.Config();
+        cfg.applyPreset(DspChain.Preset.CLEAR_ALL);
+        DspChain.EffectSlot limSlot =
+                cfg.addOrFocusSlot(DspChain.EffectType.BRICKWALL_LIMITER);
+        limSlot.value = 80;
+        limSlot.extraParams.put("drive", 10);
+
+        DspChain chain = new DspChain(1, cfg);
+        AudioProcessor lim = chain.getRack().getProcessor(
+                chain.getRack().indexOf("limiter"));
+        assertNotNull(lim);
+        assertEquals(80, lim.getParam("drive").getCurrentRaw());
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    public void safetyLimiterCeilingHoldsEvenWhenLimiterSlotMixIsZeroOrUnchecked() {
+        // BUG-03 regression: upstream gain stages (Warmth + Presence + Comp)
+        // must never clip past the brickwall safety ceiling even if a
+        // BRICKWALL_LIMITER slot has wetDry < 1.0f or is unchecked.
+        DspChain.Config cfg = new DspChain.Config();
+        cfg.applyPreset(DspChain.Preset.CLEAR_ALL);
+        DspChain.EffectSlot w = cfg.addOrFocusSlot(DspChain.EffectType.WARMTH_BODY);
+        DspChain.EffectSlot p = cfg.addOrFocusSlot(DspChain.EffectType.VOCAL_PRESENCE);
+        DspChain.EffectSlot c = cfg.addOrFocusSlot(DspChain.EffectType.COMPRESSOR);
+        DspChain.EffectSlot l = cfg.addOrFocusSlot(DspChain.EffectType.BRICKWALL_LIMITER);
+        w.value = 100;
+        p.value = 100;
+        c.value = 100;
+        l.value = 50;
+        l.wetDry = 0.0f; // 0% drive mix on limiter slot
+
+        DspChain chain = new DspChain(1, cfg);

+        chain.processInPlace(hot, 1024);
+        for (int i = 0; i < hot.length; i++) {
+            assertTrue("Sample clipped past safety ceiling @" + i + ": " + hot[i],
+                    Math.abs(hot[i]) <= 0.99f);
+        }
+    }

+    @Test
+    public void removingSlotResetsProcessorWetDryMixToDefault() {
+        // BUG-04 regression: removing a slot with wetDry = 0.2f must reset the
+        // backing processor's wetDryMix back to 1.0f on updateConfig.
+        DspChain.Config cfg = new DspChain.Config();
+        cfg.applyPreset(DspChain.Preset.VOICE_POLISH);
+        DspChain.EffectSlot noiseSlot = null;
+        for (DspChain.EffectSlot s : cfg.slots) {
+            if (s.type == DspChain.EffectType.NOISE_SUPPRESSION) noiseSlot = s;
+        }

+                chain.getRack().indexOf("noise"));

+        chain.updateConfig(cfg);

+    }

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/hello/AudioProcessor.java

package com.example.hello;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import org.json.JSONArray;
import org.json.JSONObject;

/**
 * Base class for every effect in the rack. A processor owns its {@link
 * FxParameter} list, its enable state and its wet/dry mix, and implements the
 * actual DSP in {@link #processInPlace}. This replaces the old single-integer
 * {@code DspChain.EffectSlot} model: each effect can now expose any number of
 * typed parameters, and the FX inspector renders them generically.
 *
 * &lt;p&gt;Subclasses that are 2-pass or change duration override
 * {@link #isOfflineOnly()} so the realtime path skips them; effects with a
 * tail (reverb/delay) report {@link #getTailFrames()}.
 */
public abstract class AudioProcessor {

    private volatile boolean enabled = true;
    private volatile float wetDryMix = 1.0f;
    private final List&lt;FxParameter&gt; parameters = new ArrayList<>();

    public abstract String getId();

    public abstract String getTitle();

    public abstract String getDescription();

    public abstract FxCategory getCategory();

    /** Configures channel count / sample rate; called before the first block. */
    public abstract void prepare(int sampleRate, int channels);

    /** Clears all filter/envelope state (start of a new edit region). */
    public abstract void reset();

    /** Processes {@code frames} interleaved frames in place. */
    public abstract void processInPlace(float[] interleaved, int frames,
                                        int channels);

    /** True for processors that need a second pass or alter duration. */
    public boolean isOfflineOnly() {
        return false;
    }

    /** Latency/tail in frames after the input ends (reverb, delay). */
    public int getTailFrames() {
        return 0;
    }

    public boolean isEnabled() {
        return enabled;
    }

    public void setEnabled(boolean en) {
        this.enabled = en;
    }

    public float getWetDryMix() {
        return wetDryMix;
    }

    public void setWetDryMix(float mix0to1) {
        this.wetDryMix = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, mix0to1));
    }

    public List&lt;FxParameter&gt; getParameters() {
        return parameters;
    }

    protected FxParameter addParameter(FxParameter p) {
        if (p != null) parameters.add(p);
        return p;
    }

    public FxParameter getParam(String id) {
        for (FxParameter p : parameters) {
            if (p.id.equals(id)) return p;
        }
        return null;
    }

    /**
     * True when this processor would alter audio if it ran (amount above the
     * engine's 0.001 gate). Used by chains to decide activeness.
     */
    public boolean isAudible() {
        return enabled;
    }

    /** Serializes enable, mix and every parameter's raw value. */
    public JSONObject saveState() {
        try {
            JSONObject o = new JSONObject();
            o.put("id", getId());
            o.put("enabled", enabled);
            o.put("mix", (double) wetDryMix);
            JSONArray a = new JSONArray();
            for (FxParameter p : parameters) a.put(p.toJson());
            o.put("params", a);
            return o;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return new JSONObject();
        }
    }

    /**
     * Restores enable, mix and parameters by id. Unknown ids are ignored so a
     * rack saved by a newer build still loads.
     */
    public void loadState(JSONObject json) {
        if (json == null) return;
        enabled = json.optBoolean("enabled", enabled);
        float m = (float) json.optDouble("mix", (double) wetDryMix);
        wetDryMix = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f, m));
        JSONArray a = json.optJSONArray("params");
        if (a == null) return;
        for (int i = 0; i < a.length(); i++) {
            JSONObject po = a.optJSONObject(i);
            if (po == null) continue;
            FxParameter mine = getParam(po.optString("id", ""));
            if (mine == null) continue;
            mine.setCurrentRaw(po.optInt("cur", mine.getCurrentRaw()));
        }
    }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/hello/FxParameter.java
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
package com.example.hello;

import java.util.Locale;

import org.json.JSONArray;
import org.json.JSONObject;

/**
 * Strongly typed, serializable parameter descriptor for an
 * {@link AudioProcessor}. A processor declares the parameters it exposes and
 * the FX inspector renders one control per parameter, so multi-knob effects
 * need no bespoke UI.
 *
 * &lt;p&gt;Values are stored as integer &quot;raw&quot; counts so that stepping, clamping and
 * persistence are exact; {@link #getFloat()} rescales by
 * {@link #scaleDivisor} (e.g. raw 350 with divisor 100 is 3.50). Display
 * formatting is scale-aware (dB suffix, enum labels) and can be overridden
 * with a {@link ValueFormatter}.
 */
public final class FxParameter {

    public enum Type { FLOAT, INT, BOOLEAN, ENUM, ACTION, CURVE_NODES }

    public enum Scale { LINEAR, LOGARITHMIC, DECIBEL, PIECEWISE_FREQ }

    /** Optional display override; when set, {@link #formatDisplay()} uses it. */
    public interface ValueFormatter {
        String format(FxParameter p, int rawValue);
    }

    public final String id;
    public final String name;
    public final String unit;
    public final Type type;
    public final Scale scale;
    public final int minRaw;
    public final int maxRaw;
    public final int stepRaw;
    public final int defaultRaw;
    public final int scaleDivisor;
    public final String[] enumLabels;

    private volatile int currentRaw;
    private volatile ValueFormatter formatter;

    private FxParameter(String id, String name, String unit, Type type,
                        Scale scale, int minRaw, int maxRaw, int stepRaw,
                        int defaultRaw, int scaleDivisor, String[] enumLabels) {
        this.id = id;
        this.name = name;
        this.unit = unit == null ? "" : unit;
        this.type = type;
        this.scale = scale;
        this.minRaw = minRaw;
        this.maxRaw = maxRaw;
        this.stepRaw = Math.max(1, stepRaw);
        this.scaleDivisor = Math.max(1, scaleDivisor);
        this.enumLabels = enumLabels;
        this.defaultRaw = Math.max(minRaw, Math.min(maxRaw, defaultRaw));
        this.currentRaw = clampRaw(defaultRaw);
    }

    /** Integer parameter in {@code unit} (e.g. " Hz", "%"). */
    public static FxParameter intParam(String id, String name, String unit,
                                       int min, int max, int step, int def) {
        return new FxParameter(id, name, unit, Type.INT, Scale.LINEAR,
                min, max, step, def, 1, null);
    }

    /** Fractional parameter stored scaled by 100 (two decimal places). */
    public static FxParameter floatParam(String id, String name, String unit,
                                         float min, float max, float def) {
        return new FxParameter(id, name, unit, Type.FLOAT, Scale.LINEAR,
                Math.round(min * 100f), Math.round(max * 100f), 1,
                Math.round(def * 100f), 100, null);
    }

    /** Decibel parameter (displays a " dB" suffix). */
    public static FxParameter dbParam(String id, String name,
                                      float minDb, float maxDb, float defDb) {
        FxParameter p = new FxParameter(id, name, " dB", Type.FLOAT,
                Scale.DECIBEL, Math.round(minDb * 100f), Math.round(maxDb * 100f),
                1, Math.round(defDb * 100f), 100, null);
        return p;
    }

    public static FxParameter boolParam(String id, String name, boolean def) {
        return new FxParameter(id, name, "", Type.BOOLEAN, Scale.LINEAR,
                0, 1, 1, def ? 1 : 0, 1, null);
    }

    public static FxParameter enumParam(String id, String name,
                                        String[] labels, int defIndex) {
        return new FxParameter(id, name, "", Type.ENUM, Scale.LINEAR,
                0, Math.max(0, labels.length - 1), 1, defIndex, 1, labels);
    }

    /** One-shot command parameter (renders as a button, holds no value). */
    public static FxParameter actionParam(String id, String name) {
        return new FxParameter(id, name, "", Type.ACTION, Scale.LINEAR,
                0, 0, 1, 0, 1, null);
    }

    public int getCurrentRaw() {
        return currentRaw;
    }

    public void setCurrentRaw(int raw) {
        this.currentRaw = clampRaw(raw);
    }

    public void resetToDefault() {
        this.currentRaw = clampRaw(defaultRaw);
    }

    public int clampRaw(int raw) {
        int v = Math.max(minRaw, Math.min(maxRaw, raw));
        if (type == Type.ENUM || type == Type.BOOLEAN || type == Type.ACTION) {
            return v;
        }
        if (stepRaw > 1 && v < maxRaw) {
            int steps = Math.round((v - minRaw) / (float) stepRaw);
            v = minRaw + steps * stepRaw;
            v = Math.max(minRaw, Math.min(maxRaw, v));
        }
        return v;
    }

    public float getFloat() {
        return currentRaw / (float) scaleDivisor;
    }

    public void setFloat(float v) {
        this.currentRaw = clampRaw(Math.round(v * scaleDivisor));
    }

    public boolean getBoolean() {
        return currentRaw != 0;
    }

    public void setBoolean(boolean b) {
        this.currentRaw = b ? 1 : 0;
    }

    public int getEnumIndex() {
        return currentRaw;
    }

    public void setEnumIndex(int idx) {
        this.currentRaw = clampRaw(idx);
    }

    public String getEnumLabel() {
        if (enumLabels == null || currentRaw < 0
                || currentRaw >= enumLabels.length) {
            return "";
        }
        return enumLabels[currentRaw];
    }

    public void setValueFormatter(ValueFormatter f) {
        this.formatter = f;
    }

    /** Human readable value: enum label, or scaled number plus unit. */
    public String formatDisplay() {
        if (formatter != null) {
            return formatter.format(this, currentRaw);
        }
        if (type == Type.BOOLEAN) {
            return currentRaw != 0 ? "On" : "Off";
        }
        if (type == Type.ENUM) {
            return getEnumLabel();
        }
        if (type == Type.ACTION) {
            return name;
        }
        if (scaleDivisor <= 1) {
            return unit.isEmpty() ? String.valueOf(currentRaw)
                    : currentRaw + unit;
        }
        double d = currentRaw / (double) scaleDivisor;
        String num;
        if (scale == Scale.DECIBEL) {
            num = String.format(Locale.US, "%+.1f", d);
        } else if (Math.abs(d) >= 10 || currentRaw % scaleDivisor == 0) {
            num = trimTrailingZeros(String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", d));
        } else {
            num = trimTrailingZeros(String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f", d));
        }
        return unit.isEmpty() ? num : num + unit;
    }

    private static String trimTrailingZeros(String s) {
        if (!s.contains(".")) return s;
        while (s.endsWith("0")) s = s.substring(0, s.length() - 1);
        if (s.endsWith(".")) s = s.substring(0, s.length() - 1);
        return s;
    }

    public JSONObject toJson() {
        try {
            JSONObject o = new JSONObject();
            o.put("id", id);
            o.put("name", name);
            o.put("unit", unit);
            o.put("type", type.name());
            o.put("scale", scale.name());
            o.put("min", minRaw);
            o.put("max", maxRaw);
            o.put("step", stepRaw);
            o.put("def", defaultRaw);
            o.put("div", scaleDivisor);
            o.put("cur", currentRaw);
            if (enumLabels != null) {
                JSONArray a = new JSONArray();
                for (String l : enumLabels) a.put(l);
                o.put("enums", a);
            }
            return o;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return new JSONObject();
        }
    }

    /** Rebuilds a parameter from {@link #toJson()} output. */
    public static FxParameter fromJson(JSONObject o) {
        if (o == null) return null;
        String id = o.optString("id", "");
        String[] enums = null;
        JSONArray ea = o.optJSONArray("enums");
        if (ea != null && ea.length() > 0) {
            enums = new String[ea.length()];
            for (int i = 0; i < enums.length; i++) enums[i] = ea.optString(i, "");
        }
        Type t;
        Scale sc;
        try {
            t = Type.valueOf(o.optString("type", Type.INT.name()));
        } catch (Exception e) {
            t = Type.INT;
        }
        try {
            sc = Scale.valueOf(o.optString("scale", Scale.LINEAR.name()));
        } catch (Exception e) {
            sc = Scale.LINEAR;
        }
        FxParameter p = new FxParameter(id, o.optString("name", ""),
                o.optString("unit", ""), t, sc,
                o.optInt("min", 0), o.optInt("max", 100),
                o.optInt("step", 1), o.optInt("def", 0),
                o.optInt("div", 1), enums);
        p.currentRaw = p.clampRaw(o.optInt("cur", p.defaultRaw));
        return p;
    }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/hello/FxRackChain.java
