package com.example.hello;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList;

import org.json.JSONArray;
import org.json.JSONObject;

/**
 * Ordered rack of {@link AudioProcessor}s. This replaces the hardcoded
 * internals of {@link DspChain}: processors run in rack order, a bypassed
 * processor is skipped, and a processor whose wet/dry mix is below 1.0 is
 * blended sample-accurately against the dry signal.
 *
 * &lt;p&gt;The legacy voice-chain stages are registered by id so a rack saved as
 * JSON can be rebuilt without knowing the concrete classes.
 */
public final class FxRackChain {

    /** Creates a fresh processor instance for JSON deserialization. */
    public interface ProcessorFactory {
        AudioProcessor create();
    }

    private static final Map<String, ProcessorFactory> REGISTRY =
            new LinkedHashMap<>();

    private static final String[] CANONICAL_LEGACY_ORDER = {
            "hpf", "noise", "gate", "tone", "deess", "comp", "limiter"
    };

    static {
        register("hpf", LegacyHpfProcessor::new);
        register("noise", LegacyNoiseProcessor::new);
        register("gate", LegacyGateProcessor::new);
        register("tone", LegacyToneProcessor::new);
        register("deess", LegacyDeEsserProcessor::new);
        register("comp", LegacyCompressorProcessor::new);
        register("limiter", LegacyLimiterProcessor::new);
    }

    /** Registers (or overrides) a processor id for JSON round-trips. */
    public static void register(String id, ProcessorFactory factory) {
        if (id == null || id.isEmpty() || factory == null) return;
        REGISTRY.put(id, factory);
    }

    /** Instantiates a registered processor, or null when unknown. */
    public static AudioProcessor createProcessor(String id) {
        ProcessorFactory f = REGISTRY.get(id);
        return f == null ? null : f.create();
    }

    private final CopyOnWriteArrayList&lt;AudioProcessor&gt; processors =
            new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
    private final int channels;
    private int sampleRate = 48000;
    private float[] scratch = new float[0];

    public FxRackChain(int channels) {
        this.channels = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, channels));
    }

    public int getChannels() {
        return channels;
    }

    public boolean addProcessor(AudioProcessor p) {
        if (p == null) return false;
        p.prepare(sampleRate, channels);
        return processors.addIfAbsent(p);
    }

    /** Removes and returns the processor at {@code idx}, or null. */
    public AudioProcessor removeProcessor(int idx) {
        if (idx < 0 || idx >= processors.size()) return null;
        return processors.remove(idx);
    }

    /** Moves a processor to a new rack position (drag reorder). */
    public boolean moveProcessor(int fromIdx, int toIdx) {
        if (fromIdx < 0 || fromIdx >= processors.size()
                || toIdx < 0 || toIdx >= processors.size()
                || fromIdx == toIdx) {
            return false;
        }
        AudioProcessor p = processors.remove(fromIdx);
        if (p == null) return false;
        processors.add(toIdx, p);
        return true;
    }

    public AudioProcessor getProcessor(int idx) {
        if (idx < 0 || idx >= processors.size()) return null;
        return processors.get(idx);
    }

    public int indexOf(String id) {
        if (id == null) return -1;
        for (int i = 0; i < processors.size(); i++) {
            if (id.equals(processors.get(i).getId())) return i;
        }
        return -1;
    }

    public int size() {
        return processors.size();
    }

    public boolean isEmpty() {
        return processors.isEmpty();
    }

    public void clear() {
        processors.clear();
    }

    public List&lt;AudioProcessor&gt; processors() {
        return processors;
    }

    /** True when any processor would alter audio. */
    public boolean isActive() {
        for (AudioProcessor p : processors) {
            if (p.isAudible()) return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    public void reset() {
        for (AudioProcessor p : processors) p.reset();
    }

    /** Longest tail (frames) reported by any enabled processor. */
    public int getTailFrames() {
        int max = 0;
        for (AudioProcessor p : processors) {
            if (!p.isEnabled()) continue;
            if (p.getTailFrames() > max) max = p.getTailFrames();
        }
        return max;
    }

    /**
     * Runs every enabled, audible processor in rack order. A processor at
     * full mix is applied in place; a partial mix is applied to a scratch
     * copy and blended back, so 0.5 mix is exactly half dry / half wet.
     * When the brickwall limiter's safety ceiling is armed, the post-blend
     * output is still clamped to the -0.2 dBFS ceiling so upstream gain
     * stages can never clip the output even if the limiter's drive mix is
     * reduced.
     */
    public void processInPlace(float[] buf, int frames) {
        if (buf == null || frames <= 0) return;
        int n = frames * channels;
        if (n > buf.length) n = buf.length;
        for (AudioProcessor p : processors) {
            if (!p.isEnabled() || p.isOfflineOnly() || !p.isAudible()) {
                continue;
            }
            float mix = p.getWetDryMix();
            if (mix >= 1.0f) {
                p.processInPlace(buf, frames, channels);
            } else if (mix > 0.0f) {
                if (scratch.length < buf.length) {
                    scratch = new float[Math.max(buf.length, n)];
                }
                System.arraycopy(buf, 0, scratch, 0, n);
                p.processInPlace(scratch, frames, channels);
                float w = mix;
                float d = 1.0f - mix;
                for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                    buf[i] = buf[i] * d + scratch[i] * w;
                }
                if (p instanceof LegacyLimiterProcessor
                        && ((LegacyLimiterProcessor) p).isSafetyOn()) {
                    ((LegacyLimiterProcessor) p).enforceSafetyCeiling(
                            buf, frames, channels);
                }
            } else if (p instanceof LegacyLimiterProcessor
                    && ((LegacyLimiterProcessor) p).isSafetyOn()) {
                ((LegacyLimiterProcessor) p).enforceSafetyCeiling(
                        buf, frames, channels);
            }
        }
    }

    public void setSampleRate(int sr) {
        if (sr <= 0) return;
        this.sampleRate = sr;
        for (AudioProcessor p : processors) p.prepare(sampleRate, channels);
    }

    public JSONObject toJson() {
        try {
            JSONObject o = new JSONObject();
            o.put("channels", channels);
            JSONArray a = new JSONArray();
            for (AudioProcessor p : processors) a.put(p.saveState());
            o.put("processors", a);
            return o;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return new JSONObject();
        }
    }

    /**
     * Rebuilds a rack from {@link #toJson()} output using the registry.
     * Unknown processor ids are skipped so a rack saved by a newer build
     * still loads.
     */
    public static FxRackChain fromJson(int channels, JSONObject json) {
        FxRackChain rack = new FxRackChain(channels);
        if (json == null) return rack;
        JSONArray a = json.optJSONArray("processors");
        if (a == null) return rack;
        for (int i = 0; i < a.length(); i++) {
            JSONObject po = a.optJSONObject(i);
            if (po == null) continue;
            AudioProcessor p = createProcessor(po.optString("id", ""));
            if (p == null) continue;
            p.loadState(po);
            rack.addProcessor(p);
        }
        return rack;
    }

    /**
     * Builds the rack that reproduces the legacy {@code DspChain} stage order
     * (HPF, noise, gate, tone, de-esser, compressor, limiter) for a config.
     * The combined warmth+presence stage stays a single processor because the
     * original engine applied them in one per-sample loop.
     */
    public static FxRackChain fromLegacyConfig(int channels, DspChain.Config cfg) {
        FxRackChain rack = new FxRackChain(channels);
        LegacyHpfProcessor hpf = new LegacyHpfProcessor();
        LegacyNoiseProcessor noise = new LegacyNoiseProcessor();
        LegacyGateProcessor gate = new LegacyGateProcessor();
        LegacyToneProcessor tone = new LegacyToneProcessor();
        LegacyDeEsserProcessor deess = new LegacyDeEsserProcessor();
        LegacyCompressorProcessor comp = new LegacyCompressorProcessor();
        LegacyLimiterProcessor limiter = new LegacyLimiterProcessor();
        rack.addProcessor(hpf);
        rack.addProcessor(noise);
        rack.addProcessor(gate);
        rack.addProcessor(tone);
        rack.addProcessor(deess);
        rack.addProcessor(comp);
        rack.addProcessor(limiter);
        rack.applyLegacyConfig(cfg);
        return rack;
    }

    /** Maps a {@link DspChain.EffectType} to its rack processor ID. */
    static String processorIdForSlotType(DspChain.EffectType type) {
        if (type == null) return null;
        switch (type) {
            case LOW_CUT_HPF:
                return "hpf";
            case NOISE_SUPPRESSION:
                return "noise";
            case ROOM_GATE:
                return "gate";
            case WARMTH_BODY:
            case VOCAL_PRESENCE:
                return "tone";
            case DE_ESSER:
                return "deess";
            case COMPRESSOR:
                return "comp";
            case BRICKWALL_LIMITER:
                return "limiter";
            default:
                return type.id;
        }
    }

    /**
     * Synchronizes the rack's processor execution order with the user's
     * {@code cfg.slots} order while preserving existing {@link AudioProcessor}
     * instances and their internal filter/envelope state. Unreferenced
     * processors keep their canonical relative order (with the safety limiter
     * anchored at the end unless explicitly moved earlier in {@code cfg.slots}).
     */
    private void syncOrderFromSlots(DspChain.Config cfg) {
        if (cfg == null || cfg.slots == null || cfg.slots.isEmpty()) return;
        Set&lt;String&gt; desiredIds = new LinkedHashSet<>();
        for (DspChain.EffectSlot s : cfg.slots) {
            if (s == null || s.type == null) continue;
            String pid = processorIdForSlotType(s.type);
            if (pid != null && indexOf(pid) >= 0) {
                desiredIds.add(pid);
            }
        }
        for (String canonId : CANONICAL_LEGACY_ORDER) {
            if (indexOf(canonId) >= 0) {
                desiredIds.add(canonId);
            }
        }
        for (AudioProcessor p : processors) {
            desiredIds.add(p.getId());
        }
        int targetIdx = 0;
        for (String pid : desiredIds) {
            int curIdx = indexOf(pid);
            if (curIdx >= 0 && curIdx != targetIdx) {
                moveProcessor(curIdx, targetIdx);
            }
            if (curIdx >= 0) {
                targetIdx++;
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Pushes a legacy {@code DspChain.Config} into the already-built legacy
     * rack in place, preserving filter/envelope state across live edits (the
     * original engine only changed amounts, never rebuilt the stages) and
     * synchronizing processor order with {@code cfg.slots}.
     */
    void applyLegacyConfig(DspChain.Config cfg) {
        if (cfg == null) {
            for (AudioProcessor p : processors) p.setEnabled(false);
            return;
        }
        cfg.syncLegacyFieldsFromSlots();
        syncOrderFromSlots(cfg);
        for (AudioProcessor p : processors) {
            if (p instanceof LegacyHpfProcessor) {
                ((LegacyHpfProcessor) p).setLegacyState(
                        cfg.hpfEnabled, cfg.hpfCutoffHz);
            } else if (p instanceof LegacyNoiseProcessor) {
                ((LegacyNoiseProcessor) p).setLegacyAmount(cfg.noisePct);
                p.setEnabled(cfg.noisePct > 0);
            } else if (p instanceof LegacyGateProcessor) {
                ((LegacyGateProcessor) p).setLegacyAmount(cfg.gatePct);
                p.setEnabled(cfg.gatePct > 0);
            } else if (p instanceof LegacyToneProcessor) {
                ((LegacyToneProcessor) p).setLegacyAmounts(
                        cfg.warmthPct, cfg.presencePct);
                p.setEnabled(cfg.warmthPct > 0 || cfg.presencePct > 0);
            } else if (p instanceof LegacyDeEsserProcessor) {
                ((LegacyDeEsserProcessor) p).setLegacyAmount(cfg.deEssPct);
                p.setEnabled(cfg.deEssPct > 0);
            } else if (p instanceof LegacyCompressorProcessor) {
                ((LegacyCompressorProcessor) p).setLegacyAmount(cfg.compPct);
                p.setEnabled(cfg.compPct > 0);
            } else if (p instanceof LegacyLimiterProcessor) {
                // The limiter doubles as the safety ceiling for every
                // gain-producing stage, exactly as the original engine did.
                boolean safety = cfg.compPct > 0 || cfg.warmthPct > 0
                        || cfg.presencePct > 0;
                ((LegacyLimiterProcessor) p).setLegacyState(
                        cfg.limiterPct, safety);
                p.setEnabled(cfg.limiterPct > 0 || safety);
            }
        }
    }
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/hello/DspChain.java
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
package com.example.hello;

import android.content.Context;
import android.content.SharedPreferences;

import org.json.JSONArray;
import org.json.JSONObject;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.EnumMap;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Pro Multi-Effect Rack & Real-Time/Offline 48 kHz Float DSP Engine.
 *
 * &lt;p&gt;All 8 effects in {@link EffectType} are 100% active, parameter-driven,
 * and audibly distinct:
 * &lt;ol&gt;
 *   &lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Low-Cut Filter (40–160 Hz)&lt;/b&gt;: Dynamic 2nd-order Butterworth HPF
 *       whose biquad coefficients recompute live from the user's Hz setting.</li>
 *   &lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Noise Suppression (0–100%)&lt;/b&gt;: 3-band adaptive Wiener floor gate
 *       with instant first-block noise calibration and up to -24 dB cut.</li>
 *   &lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Expander / Room Gate (0–100%)&lt;/b&gt;: Dedicated downward expander that
 *       attenuates room noise and breaths below threshold (-52..-18 dBFS).</li>
 *   &lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Warmth &amp; Chest Body (0–100%)&lt;/b&gt;: 180 Hz low-mid contour boost (up
 *       to +9 dB) for rich broadcast fullness.</li>
 *   &lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Vocal Presence &amp; Air (0–100%)&lt;/b&gt;: 3.5 kHz articulation &amp; air shelf
 *       boost (up to +10 dB) for crisp intelligibility.</li>
 *   &lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;De-Esser (4.5–9.5 kHz)&lt;/b&gt;: Split-band relative sibilance suppressor
 *       (up to -14 dB cut on harsh 'S'/'T' transients).</li>
 *   &lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Compressor &amp; Leveler (0–100%)&lt;/b&gt;: Soft-knee vocal compressor (up to
 *       6:1 ratio) with automatic makeup gain.</li>
 *   &lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;Brickwall Ceiling Limiter (0–100%)&lt;/b&gt;: Dedicated gain drive (up to
 *       +12 dB) into a zero-overshoot -0.2 dBFS soft-clip ceiling.</li>
 * &lt;/ol&gt;
 */
public final class DspChain {

    /** Session sample rate every legacy stage is tuned for. */
    public static final int SAMPLE_RATE = 48000;

    /** Catalog of available effects for the searchable "+ Add Effect" menu. */
    public enum EffectType {
        NOISE_SUPPRESSION("noise", "Noise Suppression",
                "3-band adaptive floor gate for hiss & background hum",
                "Intensity", 0, 100, 5, 50, "%"),
        DE_ESSER("deess", "De-Esser (4.5–9.5 kHz)",
                "Split-band sibilance control for harsh 'S' & 'T' sounds",
                "Reduction", 0, 100, 5, 45, "%"),
        COMPRESSOR("comp", "Compressor & Leveler",
                "Soft-knee vocal leveler with auto makeup gain",
                "Compression", 0, 100, 5, 40, "%"),
        LOW_CUT_HPF("hpf", "Low-Cut Rumble Filter",
                "2nd-order Butterworth high-pass filter for mic rumble & plosives",
                "Cutoff", 40, 160, 10, 80, " Hz"),
        VOCAL_PRESENCE("presence", "Vocal Presence & Air",
                "Clarity boost in the 3.5 kHz articulation band",
                "Boost", 0, 100, 5, 45, "%"),
        WARMTH_BODY("warmth", "Warmth & Chest Body",
                "Rich low-mid contour around 180 Hz for thin mics",
                "Warmth", 0, 100, 5, 40, "%"),
        ROOM_GATE("gate", "Expander / Room Gate",
                "Attenuates room echo and breaths between phrases",
                "Threshold", 0, 100, 5, 40, "%"),
        BRICKWALL_LIMITER("limiter", "Brickwall Ceiling Limiter",
                " Loudness drive + zero-overshoot ceiling to prevent peaking",
                "Drive", 0, 100, 5, 50, "%");

        public final String id;
        public final String title;
        public final String description;
        public final String paramLabel;
        public final int minVal;
        public final int maxVal;
        public final int stepVal;
        public final int defaultVal;
        public final String unit;

        EffectType(String id, String title, String description,
                   String paramLabel, int minVal, int maxVal, int stepVal,
                   int defaultVal, String unit) {
            this.id = id;
            this.title = title;
            this.description = description;
            this.paramLabel = paramLabel;
            this.minVal = minVal;
            this.maxVal = maxVal;
            this.stepVal = stepVal;
            this.defaultVal = defaultVal;
            this.unit = unit;
        }

        public static EffectType byId(String id) {
            for (EffectType t : values()) {
                if (t.id.equals(id)) return t;
            }
            return NOISE_SUPPRESSION;
        }
    }

    /** Premade templates of effects and their settings for the Presets dropdown. */
    public enum Preset {
        CUSTOM("custom", "Custom Rack"),
        VOICE_POLISH("voice_polish", "Voice Polish (Default Pro)"),
        PODCAST_BROADCAST("podcast", "Podcast Broadcast Master"),
        NOISY_ROOM("noisy_room", "Noisy Room Cleanup"),
        WARM_VOCAL("warm_vocal", "Warm Studio Vocal"),
        CRISP_CLARITY("crisp", "Crisp Articulation"),
        CLEAR_ALL("clear", "Flat / No Effects");

        public final String id;
        public final String title;

        Preset(String id, String title) {
            this.id = id;
            this.title = title;
        }
    }

    /** One effect pill in the user's scrollable FX rack. */
    public static final class EffectSlot {
        public final EffectType type;
        /** Checkmark state: only applied when enabled == true. */
        public boolean enabled;
        /** Current parameter setting (e.g. 0..100% or 40..160 Hz). */
        public int value;
        /** UI state: whether the collapsible settings drawer is expanded. */
        public boolean expanded;
        /** Per-slot wet/dry mix, 0..1 (1 = fully wet, the legacy default). */
        public float wetDry = 1f;
        /**
         * Secondary processor parameters persisted per slot, keyed by
         * {@link FxParameter#id} (Stage 3+ effects; the legacy stages have
         * only their primary knob, so this stays empty for them).
         */
        public final Map<String, Integer> extraParams = new LinkedHashMap<>();

        public EffectSlot(EffectType type, boolean enabled, int value, boolean expanded) {
            this.type = type;
            this.enabled = enabled;
            this.value = Math.max(type.minVal, Math.min(type.maxVal, value));
            this.expanded = expanded;
        }
    }

    /** Configuration holding both the Pro EffectSlot list and legacy fields. */
    public static final class Config {
        public static final int MODE_OFF = 0;
        public static final int MODE_NOISE_SUPPRESSION = 1;
        public static final int MODE_CUSTOM = 2;

        public int mode = MODE_OFF;
        public boolean hpfEnabled = false;
        public int hpfCutoffHz = 80;
        public int noisePct = 0;
        public int deEssPct = 0;
        public int compPct = 0;
        public int presencePct = 0;
        public int warmthPct = 0;
        public int gatePct = 0;
        public int limiterPct = 0;

        public boolean customHpf = true;
        public int customNoisePct = 35;
        public int customDeEssPct = 30;
        public int customCompPct = 30;

        public String activePresetId = Preset.VOICE_POLISH.id;
        public final List<EffectSlot> slots = new ArrayList<>();

        public Config() {
            syncSlotsFromLegacyFields();
        }

        public Config(boolean hpf, int noise, int deEss, int comp) {
            this.hpfEnabled = hpf;
            this.hpfCutoffHz = 80;
            this.noisePct = clamp(noise);
            this.deEssPct = clamp(deEss);
            this.compPct = clamp(comp);
            if (!isActive()) {
                this.mode = MODE_OFF;
            } else if (hpf && this.noisePct == 50
                    && this.deEssPct == 45 && this.compPct == 40) {
                this.mode = MODE_NOISE_SUPPRESSION;
            } else {
                this.mode = MODE_CUSTOM;
                this.customHpf = hpf;
                this.customNoisePct = this.noisePct;
                this.customDeEssPct = this.deEssPct;
                this.customCompPct = this.compPct;
            }
            syncSlotsFromLegacyFields();
        }

        /** Populates the rack with a premade template of effects and settings. */
        public void applyPreset(Preset preset) {
            slots.clear();
            activePresetId = preset.id;
            switch (preset) {
                case VOICE_POLISH:
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.LOW_CUT_HPF, true, 80, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.NOISE_SUPPRESSION, true, 50, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.DE_ESSER, true, 45, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.COMPRESSOR, true, 40, false));
                    break;
                case PODCAST_BROADCAST:
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.LOW_CUT_HPF, true, 90, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.NOISE_SUPPRESSION, true, 45, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.ROOM_GATE, true, 40, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.WARMTH_BODY, true, 45, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.VOCAL_PRESENCE, true, 35, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.DE_ESSER, true, 50, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.COMPRESSOR, true, 55, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.BRICKWALL_LIMITER, true, 55, false));
                    break;
                case NOISY_ROOM:
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.LOW_CUT_HPF, true, 110, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.NOISE_SUPPRESSION, true, 80, true));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.ROOM_GATE, true, 55, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.DE_ESSER, true, 40, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.COMPRESSOR, true, 35, false));
                    break;
                case WARM_VOCAL:
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.LOW_CUT_HPF, true, 70, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.WARMTH_BODY, true, 55, true));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.VOCAL_PRESENCE, true, 35, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.DE_ESSER, true, 40, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.COMPRESSOR, true, 45, false));
                    break;
                case CRISP_CLARITY:
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.LOW_CUT_HPF, true, 90, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.NOISE_SUPPRESSION, true, 35, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.VOCAL_PRESENCE, true, 65, true));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.DE_ESSER, true, 55, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.COMPRESSOR, true, 45, false));
                    break;
                case CLEAR_ALL:
                    break;
                case CUSTOM:
                default:
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.NOISE_SUPPRESSION, true, customNoisePct, true));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.DE_ESSER, true, customDeEssPct, false));
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.COMPRESSOR, true, customCompPct, false));
                    break;
            }
            syncLegacyFieldsFromSlots();
        }

        public EffectSlot addOrFocusSlot(EffectType type) {
            for (EffectSlot s : slots) {
                if (s.type == type) {
                    s.enabled = true;
                    s.expanded = true;
                    activePresetId = Preset.CUSTOM.id;
                    syncLegacyFieldsFromSlots();
                    return s;
                }
            }
            EffectSlot created = new EffectSlot(type, true, type.defaultVal, true);
            slots.add(created);
            activePresetId = Preset.CUSTOM.id;
            syncLegacyFieldsFromSlots();
            return created;
        }

        public void removeSlot(EffectSlot slot) {
            slots.remove(slot);
            activePresetId = Preset.CUSTOM.id;
            syncLegacyFieldsFromSlots();
        }

        public void syncLegacyFieldsFromSlots() {
            hpfEnabled = false;
            hpfCutoffHz = 80;
            noisePct = 0;
            deEssPct = 0;
            compPct = 0;
            presencePct = 0;
            warmthPct = 0;
            gatePct = 0;
            limiterPct = 0;
            for (EffectSlot s : slots) {
                if (!s.enabled) continue;
                switch (s.type) {
                    case LOW_CUT_HPF:
                        hpfEnabled = s.value > 0;
                        hpfCutoffHz = Math.max(40, Math.min(160, s.value));
                        break;
                    case NOISE_SUPPRESSION:
                        noisePct = clamp(s.value);
                        break;
                    case DE_ESSER:
                        deEssPct = clamp(s.value);
                        break;
                    case COMPRESSOR:
                        compPct = clamp(s.value);
                        break;
                    case VOCAL_PRESENCE:
                        presencePct = clamp(s.value);
                        break;
                    case WARMTH_BODY:
                        warmthPct = clamp(s.value);
                        break;
                    case ROOM_GATE:
                        gatePct = clamp(s.value);
                        break;
                    case BRICKWALL_LIMITER:
                        limiterPct = clamp(s.value);
                        break;
                }
            }
            if (!isActive()) {
                mode = MODE_OFF;
            } else if (hpfEnabled && hpfCutoffHz == 80
                    && noisePct == 50 && deEssPct == 45 && compPct == 40
                    && presencePct == 0 && warmthPct == 0 && gatePct == 0 && limiterPct == 0) {
                mode = MODE_NOISE_SUPPRESSION;
            } else {
                mode = MODE_CUSTOM;
            }
        }

        private void syncSlotsFromLegacyFields() {
            Map<EffectType, EffectSlot> existingByType =
                    new EnumMap<>(EffectType.class);
            List<EffectType> previousOrder = new ArrayList<>(slots.size());
            for (EffectSlot s : slots) {
                if (s != null && s.type != null
                        && !existingByType.containsKey(s.type)) {
                    existingByType.put(s.type, s);
                    previousOrder.add(s.type);
                }
            }
            Map<EffectType, Integer> activeValues =
                    new LinkedHashMap<>();
            if (hpfEnabled) {
                activeValues.put(EffectType.LOW_CUT_HPF,
                        Math.max(40, Math.min(160, hpfCutoffHz)));
            }
            if (noisePct > 0) {
                activeValues.put(EffectType.NOISE_SUPPRESSION, noisePct);
            }
            if (gatePct > 0) {
                activeValues.put(EffectType.ROOM_GATE, gatePct);
            }
            if (warmthPct > 0) {
                activeValues.put(EffectType.WARMTH_BODY, warmthPct);
            }
            if (presencePct > 0) {
                activeValues.put(EffectType.VOCAL_PRESENCE, presencePct);
            }
            if (deEssPct > 0) {
                activeValues.put(EffectType.DE_ESSER, deEssPct);
            }
            if (compPct > 0) {
                activeValues.put(EffectType.COMPRESSOR, compPct);
            }
            if (limiterPct > 0) {
                activeValues.put(EffectType.BRICKWALL_LIMITER, limiterPct);
            }

            slots.clear();
            // Preserve user's relative ordering and per-slot wetDry/extraParams
            // for any slots that were already present in the rack.
            for (EffectType t : previousOrder) {
                Integer val = activeValues.remove(t);
                if (val != null) {
                    EffectSlot kept = existingByType.get(t);
                    kept.enabled = true;
                    kept.value = Math.max(t.minVal, Math.min(t.maxVal, val));
                    slots.add(kept);
                }
            }
            // Append any newly enabled legacy stages in canonical stage order.
            for (Map.Entry<EffectType, Integer> e : activeValues.entrySet()) {
                EffectType t = e.getKey();
                int val = e.getValue();
                EffectSlot kept = existingByType.get(t);
                if (kept != null) {
                    kept.enabled = true;
                    kept.value = Math.max(t.minVal, Math.min(t.maxVal, val));
                    slots.add(kept);
                } else {
                    slots.add(new EffectSlot(t, true, val, false));
                }
            }
        }

        public void applyMode(int targetMode) {
            if (targetMode == MODE_OFF) {
                this.mode = MODE_OFF;
                this.hpfEnabled = false;
                this.noisePct = 0;
                this.deEssPct = 0;
                this.compPct = 0;
                this.presencePct = 0;
                this.warmthPct = 0;
                this.gatePct = 0;
                this.limiterPct = 0;
                for (EffectSlot s : slots) s.enabled = false;
            } else if (targetMode == MODE_NOISE_SUPPRESSION) {
                this.mode = MODE_NOISE_SUPPRESSION;
                this.hpfEnabled = true;
                this.hpfCutoffHz = 80;
                this.noisePct = 50;
                this.deEssPct = 45;
                this.compPct = 40;
                this.presencePct = 0;
                this.warmthPct = 0;
                this.gatePct = 0;
                this.limiterPct = 0;
                syncSlotsFromLegacyFields();
            } else {
                this.mode = MODE_CUSTOM;
                if (!customHpf && customNoisePct <= 0
                        && customDeEssPct <= 0 && customCompPct <= 0) {
                    customHpf = true;
                    customNoisePct = 35;
                    customDeEssPct = 30;
                    customCompPct = 30;
                }
                this.hpfEnabled = customHpf;
                this.noisePct = clamp(customNoisePct);
                this.deEssPct = clamp(customDeEssPct);
                this.compPct = clamp(customCompPct);
                syncSlotsFromLegacyFields();
            }
        }

        public void onManualAdjustment() {
            onManualAdjustment(hpfEnabled, noisePct, deEssPct, compPct);
        }

        public void onManualAdjustment(boolean hpf, int noise, int deEss, int comp) {
            this.hpfEnabled = hpf;
            this.noisePct = clamp(noise);
            this.deEssPct = clamp(deEss);
            this.compPct = clamp(comp);
            if (!isActive()) {
                this.mode = MODE_OFF;
            } else {
                this.mode = MODE_CUSTOM;
                this.customHpf = this.hpfEnabled;
                this.customNoisePct = this.noisePct;
                this.customDeEssPct = this.deEssPct;
                this.customCompPct = this.compPct;
            }
            syncSlotsFromLegacyFields();
        }

        public boolean isActive() {
            return hpfEnabled || noisePct > 0 || deEssPct > 0 || compPct > 0
                    || presencePct > 0 || warmthPct > 0 || gatePct > 0 || limiterPct > 0;
        }

        public int enabledSlotCount() {
            int n = 0;
            for (EffectSlot s : slots) {
                if (s.enabled && s.value > 0) n++;
            }
            return n;
        }

        public String badgeSummary() {
            if (!isActive() || mode == MODE_OFF) return "FX Off";
            if (mode == MODE_NOISE_SUPPRESSION
                    || (hpfEnabled && hpfCutoffHz == 80
                    && noisePct == 50 && deEssPct == 45 && compPct == 40
                    && presencePct == 0 && warmthPct == 0 && gatePct == 0 && limiterPct == 0)) {
                return "FX • Polish";
            }
            return "FX • Custom";
        }

        public static Config voicePolish() {
            Config c = new Config(true, 50, 45, 40);
            c.mode = MODE_NOISE_SUPPRESSION;
            return c;
        }

        public static Config load(Context ctx) {
            Config c = new Config();
            if (ctx == null) return c;
            try {
                SharedPreferences p = ctx.getSharedPreferences("takeone",
                        Context.MODE_PRIVATE);
                c.hpfEnabled = p.getBoolean("dsp_hpf", false);
                c.hpfCutoffHz = Math.max(40, Math.min(160, p.getInt("dsp_hpf_hz", 80)));
                c.noisePct = clamp(p.getInt("dsp_noise", 0));
                c.deEssPct = clamp(p.getInt("dsp_deess", 0));
                c.compPct = clamp(p.getInt("dsp_comp", 0));
                c.customHpf = p.getBoolean("dsp_custom_hpf", true);
                c.customNoisePct = clamp(p.getInt("dsp_custom_noise", 35));
                c.customDeEssPct = clamp(p.getInt("dsp_custom_deess", 30));
                c.customCompPct = clamp(p.getInt("dsp_custom_comp", 30));
                c.activePresetId = p.getString("dsp_preset_id", Preset.VOICE_POLISH.id);

                String slotsJson = p.getString("dsp_slots_json", null);
                if (slotsJson != null && !slotsJson.isEmpty()) {
                    c.slots.clear();
                    JSONArray arr = new JSONArray(slotsJson);
                    for (int i = 0; i < arr.length(); i++) {
                        JSONObject o = arr.getJSONObject(i);
                        EffectType t = EffectType.byId(o.optString("id", "noise"));
                        boolean en = o.optBoolean("en", true);
                        int val = o.optInt("val", t.defaultVal);
                        boolean exp = o.optBoolean("exp", false);
                        EffectSlot slot = new EffectSlot(t, en, val, exp);
                        slot.wetDry = Math.max(0f, Math.min(1f,
                                (float) o.optDouble("mix", 1.0)));
                        JSONObject pj = o.optJSONObject("params");
                        if (pj != null) {
                            java.util.Iterator<String> keys = pj.keys();
                            while (keys.hasNext()) {
                                String k = keys.next();
                                slot.extraParams.put(k, pj.optInt(k));
                            }
                        }
                        c.slots.add(slot);
                    }
                    c.syncLegacyFieldsFromSlots();
                } else {
                    c.syncSlotsFromLegacyFields();
                    if (c.slots.isEmpty()) {
                        // Seed default starter pills for the UI rack
                        c.slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.NOISE_SUPPRESSION, false, 50, false));
                        c.slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.DE_ESSER, false, 45, false));
                        c.slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.COMPRESSOR, false, 40, false));
                        c.slots.add(new EffectSlot(EffectType.LOW_CUT_HPF, false, 80, false));
                    }
                }
                int savedMode = p.getInt("dsp_mode", -1);
                if (savedMode >= MODE_OFF && savedMode <= MODE_CUSTOM) {
                    c.mode = savedMode;
                    if (!c.isActive()) c.mode = MODE_OFF;
                }
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            return c;
        }

        public void save(Context ctx) {
            if (ctx == null) return;
            try {
                JSONArray arr = new JSONArray();
                for (EffectSlot s : slots) {
                    JSONObject o = new JSONObject();
                    o.put("id", s.type.id);
                    o.put("en", s.enabled);
                    o.put("val", s.value);
                    o.put("exp", s.expanded);
                    o.put("mix", (double) s.wetDry);
                    if (!s.extraParams.isEmpty()) {
                        JSONObject p = new JSONObject();
                        for (Map.Entry<String, Integer> e
                                : s.extraParams.entrySet()) {
                            p.put(e.getKey(), e.getValue());
                        }
                        o.put("params", p);
                    }
                    arr.put(o);
                }
                ctx.getSharedPreferences("takeone", Context.MODE_PRIVATE).edit()
                        .putInt("dsp_mode", mode)
                        .putBoolean("dsp_hpf", hpfEnabled)
                        .putInt("dsp_hpf_hz", hpfCutoffHz)
                        .putInt("dsp_noise", clamp(noisePct))
                        .putInt("dsp_deess", clamp(deEssPct))
                        .putInt("dsp_comp", clamp(compPct))
                        .putBoolean("dsp_custom_hpf", customHpf)
                        .putInt("dsp_custom_noise", clamp(customNoisePct))
                        .putInt("dsp_custom_deess", clamp(customDeEssPct))
                        .putInt("dsp_custom_comp", clamp(customCompPct))
                        .putString("dsp_preset_id", activePresetId)
                        .putString("dsp_slots_json", arr.toString())
                        .apply();
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }

        private static int clamp(int v) {
            return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, v));
        }
    }

    private final int channels;
    private final FxRackChain rack;

    public DspChain(int channels, Config cfg) {
        this.channels = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, channels));
        this.rack = FxRackChain.fromLegacyConfig(this.channels, cfg);
        applySlotExtras(cfg);
    }

    /**
     * The modular processor rack backing this chain (Stage 2 architecture).
     * The legacy engine's eight stages are now {@link AudioProcessor}s in an
     * ordered rack; this chain is a backwards-compatible facade.
     */
    public FxRackChain getRack() {
        return rack;
    }

    public void updateConfig(Config cfg) {
        resetProcessorSlotExtras();
        rack.applyLegacyConfig(cfg);
        applySlotExtras(cfg);
    }

    /**
     * Resets per-slot wet/dry mixes and secondary parameters on all rack
     * processors before applying the active {@link Config} so removed or
     * reset slots never leave stale state behind.
     */
    private void resetProcessorSlotExtras() {
        for (AudioProcessor p : rack.processors()) {
            p.setWetDryMix(1.0f);
            for (FxParameter fp : p.getParameters()) {
                if (!isPrimaryLegacyParam(fp.id)) {
                    fp.resetToDefault();
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private static boolean isPrimaryLegacyParam(String paramId) {
        return "amt".equals(paramId)
                || "cutoff".equals(paramId)
                || "drive".equals(paramId)
                || "warmth".equals(paramId)
                || "presence".equals(paramId);
    }

    /**
     * Pushes per-slot wet/dry mixes and any persisted secondary parameters
     * into the rack. The combined warmth+presence stage is one processor fed
     * by two slots, so it has no slot-level mix and stays fully wet.
     */
    private void applySlotExtras(Config cfg) {
        if (cfg == null) return;
        for (DspChain.EffectSlot s : cfg.slots) {
            AudioProcessor p = processorForSlot(s.type);
            if (p == null) continue;
            // When the Brickwall Limiter slot is unchecked, it may still run
            // as the automatic safety ceiling for upstream gain stages; keep
            // its mix at 100% when disabled so an unchecked slot's mix never
            // weakens safety limiting.
            if (s.type != EffectType.BRICKWALL_LIMITER || s.enabled) {
                p.setWetDryMix(s.wetDry);
            }
            for (Map.Entry<String, Integer> e : s.extraParams.entrySet()) {
                if (isPrimaryLegacyParam(e.getKey())) continue;
                FxParameter fp = p.getParam(e.getKey());
                if (fp != null) fp.setCurrentRaw(e.getValue());
            }
        }
    }

    private AudioProcessor processorForSlot(EffectType type) {
        switch (type) {
            case LOW_CUT_HPF:
                return rack.getProcessor(rack.indexOf("hpf"));
            case NOISE_SUPPRESSION:
                return rack.getProcessor(rack.indexOf("noise"));
            case ROOM_GATE:
                return rack.getProcessor(rack.indexOf("gate"));
            case DE_ESSER:
                return rack.getProcessor(rack.indexOf("deess"));
            case COMPRESSOR:
                return rack.getProcessor(rack.indexOf("comp"));
            case BRICKWALL_LIMITER:
                return rack.getProcessor(rack.indexOf("limiter"));
            case WARMTH_BODY:
            case VOCAL_PRESENCE:
            default:
                // Combined tone stage (or unknown type): no slot-level mix.
                return null;
        }
    }

    public boolean isActive() {
        return rack.isActive();
    }

    public void reset() {
        rack.reset();
    }

    public void processInPlace(float[] buf, int frames) {
        rack.processInPlace(buf, frames);
    }

    public static float fastLinToDb(float x) {
        if (x <= 1e-7f) return -140.0f;
        int bits = Float.floatToRawIntBits(x);
        int exp = ((bits >>> 23) & 0xFF) - 127;
        float m = Float.intBitsToFloat((bits & 0x007FFFFF) | 0x3F800000);
        float log2m = -0.34484843f * m * m + 2.02466578f * m - 1.67487759f;
        return (exp + log2m) * 6.0205999f;
    }

    public static float fastDbToLin(float db) {
        if (db <= -140.0f) return 0.0f;
        if (db > 36.0f) db = 36.0f;
        float p = db * 0.16609640f;
        int exp = (int) Math.floor(p);
        float f = p - exp;
        float mant = 0.33971f * f * f + 0.66029f * f + 1.0f;
        int bits = Float.floatToRawIntBits(mant);
        bits += (exp << 23);
        return Float.intBitsToFloat(bits);
    }
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
package com.example.hello;

/**
 * Stage 1 of the legacy voice chain: dynamic Butterworth high-pass filter
 * (40..160 Hz). The biquad is Direct Form I, coefficients are recomputed only
 * when the cutoff or sample rate changes, and the body is a verbatim
 * extraction of the original {@code DspChain} stage so output stays
 * bit-identical.
 */
final class LegacyHpfProcessor extends AudioProcessor {

    private static final int DECIM = 16;

    private final FxParameter cutoff = addParameter(FxParameter.intParam(
            "cutoff", "Cutoff", " Hz", 40, 160, 1, 80));

    private volatile float b0, b1, b2, a1, a2;
    private volatile int currentHz = -1;
    private volatile int currentSampleRate = DspChain.SAMPLE_RATE;
    private final float[] z1 = new float[2];
    private final float[] z2 = new float[2];

    LegacyHpfProcessor() {
        computeCoefficients(80, DspChain.SAMPLE_RATE);
    }

    private void computeCoefficients(int cutoffHz, int sr) {
        int hz = Math.max(40, Math.min(160, cutoffHz));
        int validSr = sr > 0 ? sr : DspChain.SAMPLE_RATE;
        if (hz == this.currentHz && validSr == this.currentSampleRate) return;
        this.currentHz = hz;
        this.currentSampleRate = validSr;
        double w0 = 2.0 * Math.PI * hz / validSr;
        double cosW0 = Math.cos(w0);
        double alpha = Math.sin(w0) / (2.0 * 0.70710678);
        double a0Inv = 1.0 / (1.0 + alpha);
        this.b0 = (float) ((1.0 + cosW0) * 0.5 * a0Inv);
        this.b1 = (float) (-(1.0 + cosW0) * a0Inv);
        this.b2 = this.b0;
        this.a1 = (float) (-2.0 * cosW0 * a0Inv);
        this.a2 = (float) ((1.0 - alpha) * a0Inv);
    }

    @Override
    public String getId() {
        return "hpf";
    }

    @Override
    public String getTitle() {
        return "Low-Cut Rumble Filter";
    }

    @Override
    public String getDescription() {
        return "2nd-order Butterworth high-pass filter for mic rumble & plosives";
    }

    @Override
    public FxCategory getCategory() {
        return FxCategory.EQUALIZER;
    }

    @Override
    public void prepare(int sampleRate, int channels) {
        int validSr = sampleRate > 0 ? sampleRate : DspChain.SAMPLE_RATE;
        computeCoefficients(cutoff.getCurrentRaw(), validSr);
    }

    @Override
    public void reset() {
        for (int c = 0; c < 2; c++) {
            z1[c] = 0f;
            z2[c] = 0f;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isAudible() {
        return isEnabled();
    }

    /** Mirrors the legacy {@code hpfOn} flag: any cutoff above 0 enables it. */
    void setLegacyState(boolean on, int cutoffHz) {
        cutoff.setCurrentRaw(on ? Math.max(40, Math.min(160, cutoffHz)) : 80);
        computeCoefficients(cutoff.getCurrentRaw(), currentSampleRate);
        setEnabled(on);
    }

    boolean isOn() {
        return isEnabled();
    }

    @Override
    public void processInPlace(float[] buf, int frames, int channels) {
        if (buf == null || frames <= 0) return;
        final int ch = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, channels));
        computeCoefficients(cutoff.getCurrentRaw(), currentSampleRate);
        int offset = 0;
        while (offset < frames) {
            int blk = Math.min(DECIM, frames - offset);

            // Stage 1: Dynamic Butterworth High-Pass Filter (40..160 Hz)
            final float fb0 = this.b0, fb1 = this.b1, fb2 = this.b2;
            final float fa1 = this.a1, fa2 = this.a2;
            for (int f = 0; f < blk; f++) {
                int idx = (offset + f) * ch;
                for (int c = 0; c < ch; c++) {
                    float x = buf[idx + c];
                    float y = fb0 * x + z1[c];
                    z1[c] = fb1 * x - fa1 * y + z2[c];
                    z2[c] = fb2 * x - fa2 * y;
                    buf[idx + c] = y;
                }
            }

            offset += blk;
        }
    }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/hello/LegacyNoiseProcessor.java
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
package com.example.hello;

/**
 * Stage 2 of the legacy voice chain: 3-band adaptive (minimum-statistics)
 * noise suppressor. Body is a verbatim extraction of the original
 * {@code DspChain} stage so output stays bit-identical, including the
 * per-DECIM-block gain smoothing.
 */
final class LegacyNoiseProcessor extends AudioProcessor {

    private static final int DECIM = 16;
    private static final float INV_DECIM = 1.0f / DECIM;
    private static final float NS_ALPHA_LOW = 0.02839f;
    private static final float NS_ALPHA_HIGH = 0.3919f;

    private final FxParameter amt = addParameter(FxParameter.intParam(
            "amt", "Intensity", "%", 0, 100, 1, 50));

    private final float[] lp1 = new float[2];
    private final float[] lp2 = new float[2];
    private final float[] env = {1e-5f, 1e-5f, 1e-5f};
    private final float[] floor = {1e-4f, 1e-4f, 1e-4f};
    private boolean seeded = false;
    private final float[] gain = {1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f};
    private final float[] targetGain = {1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f};
    private final float[] bandAcc = new float[3];
    private final float[] scratchLow = new float[DECIM * 2];
    private final float[] scratchMid = new float[DECIM * 2];
    private final float[] scratchHigh = new float[DECIM * 2];

    @Override
    public String getId() {
        return "noise";
    }

    @Override
    public String getTitle() {
        return "Noise Suppression";
    }

    @Override
    public String getDescription() {
        return "3-band adaptive floor gate for hiss & background hum";
    }

    @Override
    public FxCategory getCategory() {
        return FxCategory.RESTORATION;
    }

    @Override
    public void prepare(int sampleRate, int channels) {
    }

    @Override
    public void reset() {
        for (int c = 0; c < 2; c++) {
            lp1[c] = 0f;
            lp2[c] = 0f;
        }
        for (int b = 0; b < 3; b++) {
            env[b] = 1e-5f;
            floor[b] = 1e-4f;
            gain[b] = 1.0f;
            targetGain[b] = 1.0f;
        }
        seeded = false;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isAudible() {
        return isEnabled() && amt01() > 0.001f;
    }

    void setLegacyAmount(int pct) {
        amt.setCurrentRaw(pct);
    }

    /** Raw amount is a percent; gain math needs a 0..1 fraction. */
    private float amt01() {
        return amt.getCurrentRaw() / 100f;
    }

    @Override
    public void processInPlace(float[] buf, int frames, int channels) {
        if (buf == null || frames <= 0) return;
        final int ch = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, channels));
        final float nAmt = amt01();
        if (nAmt <= 0.001f) return;

        int offset = 0;
        while (offset < frames) {
            int blk = Math.min(DECIM, frames - offset);

            // Stage 2: 3-Band Adaptive Noise Suppressor
            bandAcc[0] = bandAcc[1] = bandAcc[2] = 0f;
            for (int f = 0; f < blk; f++) {
                int idx = (offset + f) * ch;
                int sIdx = f * ch;
                for (int c = 0; c < ch; c++) {
                    float x = buf[idx + c];
                    lp1[c] += NS_ALPHA_LOW * (x - lp1[c]);
                    lp2[c] += NS_ALPHA_HIGH * (x - lp2[c]);
                    float bLow = lp1[c];
                    float bMid = lp2[c] - bLow;
                    float bHigh = x - lp2[c];
                    scratchLow[sIdx + c] = bLow;
                    scratchMid[sIdx + c] = bMid;
                    scratchHigh[sIdx + c] = bHigh;
                    bandAcc[0] += bLow * bLow;
                    bandAcc[1] += bMid * bMid;
                    bandAcc[2] += bHigh * bHigh;
                }
            }
            float invSamples = 1.0f / (blk * ch);
            if (!seeded) {
                seeded = true;
                for (int b = 0; b < 3; b++) {
                    float e = Math.max(1e-7f, bandAcc[b] * invSamples);
                    env[b] = e;
                    floor[b] = Math.max(1e-6f, e * 0.35f);
                }
            }
            for (int b = 0; b < 3; b++) {
                float e = bandAcc[b] * invSamples;
                env[b] = 0.60f * env[b] + 0.40f * e;
                if (env[b] < floor[b]) {
                    floor[b] = 0.75f * floor[b] + 0.25f * env[b];
                } else {
                    floor[b] = floor[b] * 1.0006f + 1e-9f;
                }
                float snr = env[b] / (floor[b] + 1e-10f);
                float overSub = 1.0f + 3.8f * nAmt;
                float g = (snr - overSub) / Math.max(snr, 1e-6f);
                float floorG = (b == 1)
                        ? (1.0f - 0.85f * nAmt)
                        : (1.0f - 0.94f * nAmt);
                g = Math.max(floorG, Math.min(1.0f, g));
                float smooth = g > targetGain[b] ? 0.40f
                        : (b == 2 ? 0.10f : 0.045f);
                targetGain[b] += smooth * (g - targetGain[b]);
            }
            float dG0 = (targetGain[0] - gain[0]) * INV_DECIM;
            float dG1 = (targetGain[1] - gain[1]) * INV_DECIM;
            float dG2 = (targetGain[2] - gain[2]) * INV_DECIM;
            for (int f = 0; f < blk; f++) {
                gain[0] += dG0;
                gain[1] += dG1;
                gain[2] += dG2;
                int idx = (offset + f) * ch;
                int sIdx = f * ch;
                for (int c = 0; c < ch; c++) {
                    buf[idx + c] = gain[0] * scratchLow[sIdx + c]
                            + gain[1] * scratchMid[sIdx + c]
                            + gain[2] * scratchHigh[sIdx + c];
                }
            }

            offset += blk;
        }
    }
}

package com.example.hello;

/**
 * Stage 3 of the legacy voice chain: dedicated downward expander / room gate.
 * Body is a verbatim extraction of the original {@code DspChain} stage so
 * output stays bit-identical.
 */
final class LegacyGateProcessor extends AudioProcessor {

    private static final int DECIM = 16;
    private static final float INV_DECIM = 1.0f / DECIM;

    private final FxParameter amt = addParameter(FxParameter.intParam(
            "amt", "Threshold", "%", 0, 100, 1, 40));

    private float env = 0.0f;
    private float gainCur = 1.0f;
    private float gainTarget = 1.0f;

    @Override
    public String getId() {
        return "gate";
    }

    @Override
    public String getTitle() {
        return "Expander / Room Gate";
    }

    @Override
    public String getDescription() {
        return "Attenuates room echo and breaths between phrases";
    }

    @Override
    public FxCategory getCategory() {
        return FxCategory.DYNAMICS;
    }

    @Override
    public void prepare(int sampleRate, int channels) {
    }

    @Override
    public void reset() {
        env = 0f;
        gainCur = 1.0f;
        gainTarget = 1.0f;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isAudible() {
        return isEnabled() && amt01() > 0.001f;
    }

    void setLegacyAmount(int pct) {
        amt.setCurrentRaw(pct);
    }

    /** Raw amount is a percent; gain math needs a 0..1 fraction. */
    private float amt01() {
        return amt.getCurrentRaw() / 100f;
    }

    @Override
    public void processInPlace(float[] buf, int frames, int channels) {
        if (buf == null || frames <= 0) return;
        final int ch = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, channels));
        final float gAmt = amt01();
        if (gAmt <= 0.001f) return;

        int offset = 0;
        while (offset < frames) {
            int blk = Math.min(DECIM, frames - offset);

            // Stage 3: Dedicated Downward Expander / Room Gate
            float blkPeak = 0f;
            for (int f = 0; f < blk; f++) {
                int idx = (offset + f) * ch;
                for (int c = 0; c < ch; c++) {
                    float a = Math.abs(buf[idx + c]);
                    if (a > blkPeak) blkPeak = a;
                }
            }
            float alphaG = blkPeak > env ? 0.35f : 0.015f;
            env += alphaG * (blkPeak - env);
            // Threshold ranges from -52 dBFS (0.0025) to -18 dBFS (0.125)
            float threshLin = DspChain.fastDbToLin(-52.0f + 34.0f * gAmt);
            if (env >= threshLin) {
                gainTarget = 1.0f;
            } else {
                float ratio = env / Math.max(1e-6f, threshLin);
                float minFloor = 1.0f - 0.92f * gAmt;
                gainTarget = Math.max(minFloor, ratio * ratio);
            }
            float dGate = (gainTarget - gainCur) * INV_DECIM;
            for (int f = 0; f < blk; f++) {
                gainCur += dGate;
                int idx = (offset + f) * ch;
                for (int c = 0; c < ch; c++) {
                    buf[idx + c] *= gainCur;
                }
            }

            offset += blk;
        }
    }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/hello/LegacyToneProcessor.java
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
package com.example.hello;

/**
 * Stage 4 of the legacy voice chain: Warmth (180 Hz chest body) &amp; Vocal
 * Presence (3.5 kHz air) applied in one pass. Both live in a single processor
 * because in the original engine they shared one per-sample loop; splitting
 * them would change the result (presence reads the warmth-blended sample).
 * Body is a verbatim extraction of the original {@code DspChain} stage.
 */
final class LegacyToneProcessor extends AudioProcessor {

    private static final int DECIM = 16;
    private static final float WARM_ALPHA = 0.0233f;
    private static final float PRES_ALPHA = 0.3678f;

    private final FxParameter warmth = addParameter(FxParameter.intParam(
            "warmth", "Warmth", "%", 0, 100, 1, 40));
    private final FxParameter presence = addParameter(FxParameter.intParam(
            "presence", "Presence", "%", 0, 100, 1, 45));

    private final float[] warmLp = new float[2];
    private final float[] presLp = new float[2];

    @Override
    public String getId() {
        return "tone";
    }

    @Override
    public String getTitle() {
        return "Warmth & Presence";
    }

    @Override
    public String getDescription() {
        return "Low-mid chest body (~180 Hz) plus vocal air & articulation (~3.5 kHz)";
    }

    @Override
    public FxCategory getCategory() {
        return FxCategory.EQUALIZER;
    }

    @Override
    public void prepare(int sampleRate, int channels) {
    }

    @Override
    public void reset() {
        for (int c = 0; c < 2; c++) {
            warmLp[c] = 0f;
            presLp[c] = 0f;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isAudible() {
        return isEnabled() && (warmth01() > 0.001f
                || presence01() > 0.001f);
    }

    void setLegacyAmounts(int warmthPct, int presencePct) {
        warmth.setCurrentRaw(warmthPct);
        presence.setCurrentRaw(presencePct);
    }

    /** Raw amounts are percents; gain math needs 0..1 fractions. */
    private float warmth01() {
        return warmth.getCurrentRaw() / 100f;
    }

    private float presence01() {
        return presence.getCurrentRaw() / 100f;
    }

    @Override
    public void processInPlace(float[] buf, int frames, int channels) {
        if (buf == null || frames <= 0) return;
        final int ch = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, channels));
        final float wAmt = warmth01();
        final float pAmt = presence01();
        if (wAmt <= 0.001f && pAmt <= 0.001f) return;

        int offset = 0;
        while (offset < frames) {
            int blk = Math.min(DECIM, frames - offset);

            // Stage 4: Warmth (180 Hz Chest Body) & Vocal Presence (3.5 kHz Air)
            float warmGain = wAmt * 1.45f; // up to +9 dB low-mid body
            float presGain = pAmt * 1.65f; // up to +10 dB air & articulation
            for (int f = 0; f < blk; f++) {
                int idx = (offset + f) * ch;
                for (int c = 0; c < ch; c++) {
                    float x = buf[idx + c];
                    warmLp[c] += WARM_ALPHA * (x - warmLp[c]);
                    presLp[c] += PRES_ALPHA * (x - presLp[c]);
                    float hi = x - presLp[c];
                    buf[idx + c] = x + warmGain * warmLp[c] + presGain * hi;
                }
            }

            offset += blk;
        }
    }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/hello/LegacyDeEsserProcessor.java
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
package com.example.hello;

/**
 * Stage 5 of the legacy voice chain: split-band relative-sidechain de-esser
 * (6.8 kHz bandpass, Q=1.5). Body is a verbatim extraction of the original
 * {@code DspChain} stage so output stays bit-identical.
 */
final class LegacyDeEsserProcessor extends AudioProcessor {

    private static final int DECIM = 16;
    private static final float INV_DECIM = 1.0f / DECIM;

    private final FxParameter amt = addParameter(FxParameter.intParam(
            "amt", "Reduction", "%", 0, 100, 1, 45));

    private volatile float b0, b1, b2, a1, a2;
    private volatile int currentSampleRate = -1;
    private final float[] z1 = new float[2];
    private final float[] z2 = new float[2];
    private float sibEnv = 1e-7f;
    private float fullEnv = 1e-6f;
    private float cutCur = 0.0f;
    private float cutTarget = 0.0f;
    private final float[] scratchSib = new float[DECIM * 2];

    LegacyDeEsserProcessor() {
        computeCoefficients(DspChain.SAMPLE_RATE);
    }

    private void computeCoefficients(int sr) {
        int validSr = sr > 0 ? sr : DspChain.SAMPLE_RATE;
        if (validSr == this.currentSampleRate) return;
        this.currentSampleRate = validSr;
        double dw0 = 2.0 * Math.PI * 6800.0 / validSr;
        double dCos = Math.cos(dw0);
        double dAlpha = Math.sin(dw0) / (2.0 * 1.5);
        double da0Inv = 1.0 / (1.0 + dAlpha);
        this.b0 = (float) (dAlpha * da0Inv);
        this.b1 = 0.0f;
        this.b2 = (float) (-dAlpha * da0Inv);
        this.a1 = (float) (-2.0 * dCos * da0Inv);
        this.a2 = (float) ((1.0 - dAlpha) * da0Inv);
    }

    @Override
    public String getId() {
        return "deess";
    }

    @Override
    public String getTitle() {
        return "De-Esser (4.5–9.5 kHz)";
    }

    @Override
    public String getDescription() {
        return "Split-band sibilance control for harsh 'S' & 'T' sounds";
    }

    @Override
    public FxCategory getCategory() {
        return FxCategory.RESTORATION;
    }

    @Override
    public void prepare(int sampleRate, int channels) {
        computeCoefficients(sampleRate);
    }

    @Override
    public void reset() {
        for (int c = 0; c < 2; c++) {
            z1[c] = 0f;
            z2[c] = 0f;
        }
        sibEnv = 1e-7f;
        fullEnv = 1e-6f;
        cutCur = 0.0f;
        cutTarget = 0.0f;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isAudible() {
        return isEnabled() && amt01() > 0.001f;
    }

    void setLegacyAmount(int pct) {
        amt.setCurrentRaw(pct);
    }

    /** Raw amount is a percent; gain math needs a 0..1 fraction. */
    private float amt01() {
        return amt.getCurrentRaw() / 100f;
    }

    @Override
    public void processInPlace(float[] buf, int frames, int channels) {
        if (buf == null || frames <= 0) return;
        final int ch = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, channels));
        final float dAmt = amt01();
        if (dAmt <= 0.001f) return;

        final float fb0 = this.b0, fb1 = this.b1, fb2 = this.b2;
        final float fa1 = this.a1, fa2 = this.a2;

        int offset = 0;
        while (offset < frames) {
            int blk = Math.min(DECIM, frames - offset);

            // Stage 5: Split-Band Relative Sidechain De-Esser
            float sibEnergy = 0f;
            float fullEnergy = 0f;
            for (int f = 0; f < blk; f++) {
                int idx = (offset + f) * ch;
                int sIdx = f * ch;
                for (int c = 0; c < ch; c++) {
                    float x = buf[idx + c];
                    float sib = fb0 * x + z1[c];
                    z1[c] = fb1 * x - fa1 * sib + z2[c];
                    z2[c] = fb2 * x - fa2 * sib;
                    scratchSib[sIdx + c] = sib;
                    sibEnergy += sib * sib;
                    fullEnergy += x * x;
                }
            }
            float invN = 1.0f / (blk * ch);
            sibEnv = 0.50f * sibEnv + 0.50f * (sibEnergy * invN);
            fullEnv = 0.70f * fullEnv + 0.30f * (fullEnergy * invN);
            float ratioDb = DspChain.fastLinToDb(sibEnv + 1e-10f) * 0.5f
                    - DspChain.fastLinToDb(fullEnv + 1e-9f) * 0.5f;
            float threshDb = 1.0f - 11.5f * dAmt;
            float wantCut = 0f;
            if (ratioDb > threshDb && fullEnv > 1e-7f) {
                float overDb = Math.min(14.0f, (ratioDb - threshDb) * 1.4f);
                wantCut = 1.0f - DspChain.fastDbToLin(-overDb);
            }
            float alphaDe = wantCut > cutTarget ? 0.50f : 0.12f;
            cutTarget += alphaDe * (wantCut - cutTarget);
            float dCut = (cutTarget - cutCur) * INV_DECIM;
            for (int f = 0; f < blk; f++) {
                cutCur += dCut;
                int idx = (offset + f) * ch;
                int sIdx = f * ch;
                for (int c = 0; c < ch; c++) {
                    buf[idx + c] -= cutCur * scratchSib[sIdx + c];
                }
            }

            offset += blk;
        }
    }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/hello/LegacyCompressorProcessor.java

package com.example.hello;

/**
 * Stage 6 of the legacy voice chain: soft-knee compressor with auto-makeup
 * gain. Body is a verbatim extraction of the original {@code DspChain} stage
 * so output stays bit-identical.
 */
final class LegacyCompressorProcessor extends AudioProcessor {

    private static final int DECIM = 16;
    private static final float INV_DECIM = 1.0f / DECIM;

    private final FxParameter amt = addParameter(FxParameter.intParam(
            "amt", "Compression", "%", 0, 100, 1, 40));

    private float envDb = -60.0f;
    private float gainCur = 1.0f;
    private float gainTarget = 1.0f;

    @Override
    public String getId() {
        return "comp";
    }

    @Override
    public String getTitle() {
        return "Compressor & Leveler";
    }

    @Override
    public String getDescription() {
        return "Soft-knee vocal leveler with auto makeup gain";
    }

    @Override
    public FxCategory getCategory() {
        return FxCategory.DYNAMICS;
    }

    @Override
    public void prepare(int sampleRate, int channels) {
    }

    @Override
    public void reset() {
        envDb = -60.0f;
        gainCur = 1.0f;
        gainTarget = 1.0f;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isAudible() {
        return isEnabled() && amt01() > 0.001f;
    }

    void setLegacyAmount(int pct) {
        amt.setCurrentRaw(pct);
    }

    /** Raw amount is a percent; gain math needs a 0..1 fraction. */
    private float amt01() {
        return amt.getCurrentRaw() / 100f;
    }

    @Override
    public void processInPlace(float[] buf, int frames, int channels) {
        if (buf == null || frames <= 0) return;
        final int ch = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, channels));
        final float cAmt = amt01();
        if (cAmt <= 0.001f) return;

        int offset = 0;
        while (offset < frames) {
            int blk = Math.min(DECIM, frames - offset);

            // Stage 6: Soft-Knee Compressor + Auto-Makeup
            float peak = 0f;
            float sumSq = 0f;
            for (int f = 0; f < blk; f++) {
                int idx = (offset + f) * ch;
                for (int c = 0; c < ch; c++) {
                    float a = Math.abs(buf[idx + c]);
                    if (a > peak) peak = a;
                    sumSq += a * a;
                }
            }
            float rms = (float) Math.sqrt(sumSq / (blk * ch));
            float det = Math.max(1e-6f, 0.7f * peak + 0.3f * rms);
            float inDb = DspChain.fastLinToDb(det);
            float alphaC = inDb > envDb ? 0.10f : 0.004f;
            envDb += alphaC * (inDb - envDb);

            float threshDb = -10.0f - 18.0f * cAmt; // -10 .. -28 dBFS
            float ratio = 1.0f + 5.0f * cAmt;       // 1:1 .. 6:1
            float slope = 1.0f - (1.0f / ratio);
            float knee = 6.0f;
            float over = envDb - threshDb;
            float grDb;
            if (2.0f * over < -knee) {
                grDb = 0.0f;
            } else if (2.0f * Math.abs(over) <= knee) {
                float t = over + knee * 0.5f;
                grDb = -slope * (t * t) / (2.0f * knee);
            } else {
                grDb = -slope * over;
            }
            float makeupDb = (-threshDb * slope) * 0.55f;
            gainTarget = DspChain.fastDbToLin(grDb + makeupDb);
            float dG = (gainTarget - gainCur) * INV_DECIM;
            for (int f = 0; f < blk; f++) {
                gainCur += dG;
                int idx = (offset + f) * ch;
                for (int c = 0; c < ch; c++) {
                    buf[idx + c] *= gainCur;
                }
            }

            offset += blk;
        }
    }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/hello/LegacyLimiterProcessor.java

package com.example.hello;

/**
 * Stage 7 of the legacy voice chain: dedicated brickwall ceiling limiter.
 * Also acts as the chain's safety limiter whenever any of the gain-producing
 * stages (compressor, warmth, presence) is active, exactly like the original
 * engine. Body is a verbatim extraction of the original {@code DspChain}
 * stage so output stays bit-identical.
 */
final class LegacyLimiterProcessor extends AudioProcessor {

    private static final int DECIM = 16;

    private final FxParameter drive = addParameter(FxParameter.intParam(
            "drive", "Drive", "%", 0, 100, 1, 50));

    /** True when a gain-producing stage upstream needs the safety ceiling. */
    private volatile boolean safetyOn = false;

    @Override
    public String getId() {
        return "limiter";
    }

    @Override
    public String getTitle() {
        return "Brickwall Ceiling Limiter";
    }

    @Override
    public String getDescription() {
        return "Loudness drive + zero-overshoot ceiling to prevent peaking";
    }

    @Override
    public FxCategory getCategory() {
        return FxCategory.DYNAMICS;
    }

    @Override
    public void prepare(int sampleRate, int channels) {
    }

    @Override
    public void reset() {
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isAudible() {
        return isEnabled() && (drive01() > 0.001f || safetyOn);
    }

    void setLegacyState(int drivePct, boolean safety) {
        drive.setCurrentRaw(drivePct);
        this.safetyOn = safety;
    }

    boolean isSafetyOn() {
        return safetyOn;
    }

    /** Raw drive is a percent; gain math needs a 0..1 fraction. */
    private float drive01() {
        return drive.getCurrentRaw() / 100f;
    }

    /**
     * Applies only the unity-gain -0.2 dBFS brickwall safety ceiling without
     * additional drive, used when the limiter's wet/dry mix is below 1.0 while
     * upstream gain stages require peak protection.
     */
    void enforceSafetyCeiling(float[] buf, int frames, int channels) {
        if (buf == null || frames <= 0) return;
        final int ch = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, channels));
        int n = Math.min(buf.length, frames * ch);
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            float y = buf[i];
            float ay = Math.abs(y);
            if (ay > 0.82f) {
                float d = ay - 0.82f;
                float lim = 0.82f + 0.165f * (d / (0.165f + d));
                buf[i] = y >= 0f ? lim : -lim;
            }
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void processInPlace(float[] buf, int frames, int channels) {
        if (buf == null || frames <= 0) return;
        final int ch = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, channels));
        final float lAmt = drive01();
        if (lAmt <= 0.001f && !safetyOn) return;

        int offset = 0;
        while (offset < frames) {
            int blk = Math.min(DECIM, frames - offset);

            // Stage 7: Dedicated Brickwall Ceiling Limiter
            float driveLin = lAmt > 0.001f
                    ? DspChain.fastDbToLin(lAmt * 10.5f) : 1.0f;
            for (int f = 0; f < blk; f++) {
                int idx = (offset + f) * ch;
                for (int c = 0; c < ch; c++) {
                    float y = buf[idx + c] * driveLin;
                    float ay = Math.abs(y);
                    if (ay > 0.82f) {
                        float d = ay - 0.82f;
                        float lim = 0.82f + 0.165f * (d / (0.165f + d));
                        y = y >= 0f ? lim : -lim;
                    }
                    buf[idx + c] = y;
                }
            }

            offset += blk;
        }
    }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/hello/TeleValue.java
